### [1] [Requirements After the First Edit: Mining Late Requirement Emergence and Rework in Real-World Coding-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03028)
*Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Jialong Li,Weixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究量化了编程代理会话中“需求在实现后才出现”导致的代码失效问题，发现此类迟到需求之后的既有代理代码删除或替换量约为普通非需求编辑后的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统需求工程已表明需求波动会造成进度与成本风险，但通常只能在发布周期层面观测。现有编程代理研究要么预先固定需求，要么仅统计用户反驳频率，尚未将迟到需求与其引发的具体代码返工联系起来。

Method: 作者分析3,553个符合条件的SWE-chat会话，对实现完成后到达的需求按三个维度编码；对于可重放仓库状态的样本，将需求到达与代理先前编写代码行的删除或替换关联，作为代码失效代理指标。研究以匹配的非需求编辑为对照，并进行用户轮次、净删除量等稳健性检验；另通过受控实验比较延迟披露与预先提示需求的影响。

Result: 迟到需求到达后，代理既有代码的删除或替换规模约为匹配非需求编辑后的两倍；该结果在用户轮次和净删除量检验下保持稳健，但不能据此证明因果关系。会话内该负担未呈现可检测的下降趋势；在进行多重比较校正后，未发现其与操作类型存在显著关联，且部分估计区间较宽。受控实验显示，延迟披露会将实现工作推迟至需求揭示之后，而预先警示并未显示出对代码覆盖量的显著影响。

Conclusion: 论文将迟到出现的需求确立为编程代理代码失效的可测量来源，说明评估和设计编码代理时应关注需求逐步显现造成的返工，而非仅使用预先固定需求的基准测试。其核心限制是观测研究中的关联证据不能直接支持因果结论，且部分细分效应估计仍不够精确。

Abstract: Coding agents often implement changes before users have fully articulated their requirements, echoing a pattern from requirements engineering: stakeholders cannot express a constraint until part of the system exists to react to. This volatility is associated with schedule and budget overruns in traditional projects, but only at release-cycle granularity. Existing work on coding agents narrows this gap only partway: curated benchmarks fix requirements before implementation by design, and observational studies report pushback frequency without linking arrivals to the code invalidation they cause. We address this using 3,553 eligible SWE-chat sessions, coding post-implementation requirement arrivals along three dimensions and, where repository state can be replayed, linking each arrival to a proxy: deletion or replacement of prior agent-authored lines. A requirement's arrival is followed by roughly twice as much invalidation as matched non-requirement edits, robust to user-turn and net-deletion checks, though not demonstrated as causal. This burden shows no detectable decline over a session and no detected association with operation type once multiplicity is accounted for; several intervals remain wide. A controlled experiment shows delayed disclosure relocates implementation post-reveal, while advance warning produces no detected effect on overwriting. These results establish late requirement emergence as a measurable source of code invalidation.

</details>


### [2] [Large Language Models and Language Server Protocol: a match made in context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03086)
*Alessandro Schena,Ilgiz Mustafin,Julia Kotovich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文介绍 Eiffel-tools：面向 Eiffel 语言的语言服务器协议实现，通过大语言模型与静态形式化验证器协同，实现代码、规约生成及自动缺陷修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使大语言模型能够生成程序代码，其输出仍可能不满足静态验证要求。该工作旨在将语言与项目上下文、结构化提示和形式化验证反馈结合起来，提高面向可静态验证软件的生成与修复可靠性。

Method: Eiffel-tools 在 LSP 环境中提供交互式与非交互式命令，用于生成代码和规约。系统构造包含程序及项目特定知识的丰富提示词，引导模型生成候选修复；随后调用静态验证器检查结果。对于未通过验证的输出，工具根据验证结果进行纠正、拒绝或重试，直至通过验证或达到尝试上限。实验在两个公开缺陷数据集上，使用三种模型和不同提示配置评估自动修复能力。

Result: 实验显示，结合大语言模型与形式化验证器后，Eiffel-tools 的缺陷修复率为76%至95%，具体表现取决于所采用的模型及提示策略。结果同时表明，允许更多修复尝试通常可提升成功率，但会带来额外的推理与验证成本。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将大语言模型置于“生成—验证—反馈—重试”的闭环中，能够显著增强其对静态验证软件的代码修复能力。Eiffel-tools 的关键贡献在于利用项目语境和形式化验证约束控制模型输出，但实际部署时仍需在修复成功率与尝试次数、计算成本之间权衡。

Abstract: This article introduces Eiffel-tools, a language server protocol (LSP) implementation for the Eiffel programming language that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to aid the development of statically verified software. The tool provides various interactive and non-interactive commands to produce code and specifications. It uses language and project specific knowledge to precisely direct the LLM and verifies the output using a static verifier. It crafts rich programmatic prompts for the input and corrects or rejects the output. Furthermore, it handles the retries until the program passes verification. The tool's bug fixing capability is evaluated on 2 public datasets using 3 models. The tool can fix 76% to 95% of bugs by combining LLMs and a formal verifier depending on the model and prompts used. The results show the trade-off between the number of fixing attempts and the success rate.

</details>


### [3] [Compound Prompt Constraints in LLM Code Generation: A Factorial Study of Format, Persona, and Urgency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03156)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Nickalsa LaPlaca,Caleb Stone,Ashok Raja,Omar Ochoa,Vidhyashree Nagaraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过3×3×3全因子实验研究输出格式、角色设定和紧迫性表述对大语言模型代码生成可靠性的联合影响，发现复合约束可能产生架构相关的超加性性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的代码生成提示通常同时包含多项约束，但单因素实验无法揭示这些约束之间的交互效应。研究旨在量化复合提示条件对代码生成可靠性的影响，并区分格式失败与推理失败。

Method: 研究覆盖27种条件组合、164道HumanEval+题目及5个模型，共进行22140次贪心解码评估。实验因素包括输出格式、角色设定和紧迫性表述；研究将复合条件分解为加性预测与交互残差，并采用格式感知提取流程、McNemar检验、优势比和95%置信区间进行分析。

Result: GPT-4o系列表现出稳定的超加性退化，pass@1相较加性预测额外下降3至12个百分点，其中GPT-4o-mini在“JSON+专家角色+中等紧迫性”条件下的最大交互效应为-12.2个百分点。JSON组合通常比XML产生更强交互效应。GPT-4.1系列总体具有较强抵抗力，而o3-mini呈现不同模式，结构化输出约束反而可能提升性能。

Conclusion: 复合提示约束的影响不能由单因素实验可靠预测，其脆弱性主要取决于模型架构而非规模。即使单独使用时中性或有益的约束，组合后也可能导致显著性能下降，因此面向工程应用的可靠性评估应将复合提示测试作为标准环节。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering pipelines for code generation, where production prompts often combine multiple constraints. This paper presents a full-factorial empirical study of how output formatting, persona assignment, and urgency framing jointly affect LLM code-generation reliability. We evaluate all 27 combinations in a controlled 3x3x3 design and decompose each compound condition into an additive prediction and a residual interaction term that captures super-additive degradation. The study uses all 164 HumanEval+ problems across five OpenAI models from the GPT-4o family, GPT-4.1 family, and o3-mini, yielding 22,140 greedy-decoding evaluations. A format-aware extraction pipeline separates formatting failures from reasoning failures, and significance is assessed with McNemar's test, odds ratios, and 95% confidence intervals.
  Results show that compound constraints can produce architecture-dependent degradation not predictable from single-factor experiments. The GPT-4o family exhibits consistent super-additive effects, with pass@1 reductions 3-12 percentage points beyond additive predictions; the largest interaction is -12.2 pp on GPT-4o-mini for JSON + expert persona + moderate urgency. JSON combinations generally produce larger interactions than XML. In contrast, the GPT-4.1 family is largely resistant, while o3-mini shows a qualitatively different pattern in which structured output constraints can improve performance. These findings show that vulnerability is architecture-dependent rather than size-dependent, that individually neutral or beneficial constraints can combine to cause substantial degradation, and that compound-prompt testing should be standard in reliability assessment for LLM-assisted engineering pipelines.

</details>


### [4] [Two Truths and A Lie? Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf LLMs for Requirements Quality Assessment: Performance, False Alarms, and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03230)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于INCOSE质量标准，对OpenAI和Anthropic两个系列、共十个现成大语言模型进行需求质量评估基准测试。结果表明，模型漏检率高、对需要系统工程判断的必要性和正确性问题尤其无力，且代际升级和采样温度变化并未带来稳定改善，因此当前LLM尚不适合作为自主需求评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程质量直接影响后续系统工程活动；自然语言需求中的缺陷若未被发现，可能导致设计返工、进度延误和成本增加。尽管LLM被认为能够自动化需求质量审查，但其实际可靠性缺乏充分的实证证据，因此有必要进行系统基准评估。

Method: 研究以基于INCOSE质量准则构建的专家标注结果为真实基准，评估两个模型家族、五个代际共十个现成LLM。在两个需求数据集上进行100次独立运行，并设置五种采样温度，重点分析问题检出率、误报率、不同质量维度的表现、模型代际差异以及采样随机性的影响。

Result: 模型整体呈现明显的不对称错误特征：表现最佳的Anthropic模型对专家识别问题的中位检出率仅为47%，误报率为11%。涉及系统工程判断的必要性和正确性问题几乎经常被漏检。模型代际性能并非单调提升，新模型不一定优于旧模型；改变采样温度仅引起幅度有限且无规律的性能变化，说明主要缺陷更可能源于模型能力本身，而非随机采样。

Conclusion: 现成LLM目前不足以承担自主需求质量评估任务。将其整合进Agentic AI等复杂架构并不必然弥补缺陷，反而可能放大错误。近期更可行且更具可辩护性的应用方式是作为人机协同决策支持工具，由领域专家进行最终判断。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) governs the quality of everything downstream in systems engineering (SE); defective requirements that survive review cycles propagate into design rework, schedule delays, and cost overruns. Because requirements are often written in natural language, recent advances in generative AI have raised expectations that large language models (LLMs) can absorb requirement quality assessment, a task otherwise slow and human expertise-intensive. Yet empirical evidence on whether LLMs can be trusted to do so remains scarce. This study presents the first benchmarking analysis of off-the-shelf LLM performance for requirement quality evaluation. Against an expert-derived ground truth built on INCOSE quality criteria, we evaluate ten models spanning two families (OpenAI and Anthropic) and five generations each, across one hundred independent runs, two requirement sets, and five sampling temperatures. Four contributions follow. First, we quantify a strongly asymmetric error profile: across all models and runs, the best-performing Anthropic model detects a median of only 47% of expert-identified issues while false-flagging 11%. Second, performance degrades significantly where SE judgment is required, as necessity and correctness issues are almost always missed. Third, generational progress is non-monotonic, so newer models cannot be assumed better. Fourth, this error behavior shifts only modestly and non-monotonically across sampling temperatures, indicating characteristic model deficiencies rather than inherent stochasticity. Off-the-shelf LLMs are therefore not yet trustworthy autonomous evaluators. Findings also warrant caution for Agentic AI developers: orchestrating these LLM modules in specialized architectures risks compounding these deficiencies rather than correcting them. Their defensible near-term role is human-in-the-loop decision support.

</details>


### [5] [Refusing the Impossible: A Taxonomy and Benchmark for Code Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03267)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Ashish Kundu,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将代码幻觉定义为缺乏现实依据的生成，并与有依据程序中的普通代码错误区分。基于涵盖六种语言、270个不可满足任务和91个可解决控制任务的对抗性评测，12个开源权重模型在约60%的不可满足任务上生成了无依据代码，仅27%选择拒答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成评测主要关注程序是否能够运行或通过测试，难以识别模型在不可能、不可满足或违背事实的任务上编造包、算法和实现。本文旨在建立代码幻觉的系统定义、分类体系和可重复的测量方法。

Method: 研究将代码幻觉形式化为ungrounded generation，并与普通代码错误区分。分类体系包含三个维度：groundedness，包括违反普适真理的绝对型和捏造生态或情境事实的相对型；manifestation level，包括语法、语义和事实层面；behavior，包括自信编造到退化输出。作者构建了覆盖六种语言、24个子类别的270个故意不可满足任务，并配套91个匹配的可解决控制任务。12个开源权重代码与推理模型共提交4332份回答，由经过人工标注验证的两层评审协议进行判断。

Result: 评审协议与人工标签达到82%的总体一致率，Cohen's kappa为0.73，表明一致性较高。模型在不可满足提示上约60%的情况下生成无依据代码，仅27%的情况下拒答；在可解决控制任务上错误拒答率为0%。这说明模型能够完成可行任务，但普遍缺乏在任务不可满足时识别限制并拒绝编造的能力。

Conclusion: 代码幻觉是独立于一般代码缺陷的重要可靠性问题，应从“能否运行”扩展到“是否有现实依据”进行评估。本文的分类体系和对抗性基准为测量该问题提供了结构化工具；结果表明，未来模型训练和评测需要强化不可满足性识别、事实 grounding、适当拒答以及对程序生态约束的验证能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce code that looks plausible but is not grounded in reality. The code may import packages that do not exist or claim to implement algorithms that violate proven theorems, while still compiling and running. We study \emph{code hallucination} as \emph{ungrounded generation} and separate it from ordinary \emph{code error} (bugs in otherwise grounded programs). We propose a taxonomy with three dimensions: \textbf{groundedness} (absolute violations of universal truths vs.\ relative fabrications of contingent or ecosystem-specific facts), \textbf{manifestation level} (syntactic, semantic, or factual), and \textbf{behavior} (from confident fabrication to degenerate output), organized into a severity ordering. We build an \textbf{adversarial} suite of deliberately unsatisfiable tasks where the correct response is to refuse and categorize the responses under our taxonomy. The suite contains \textbf{270 prompts} across six languages and 24 subcategories, paired with \textbf{91 matched solvable controls}, and responses are judged by a two-tier protocol validated against human labels (82\% agreement, $κ{=}0.73$). Across twelve open-weight code and reasoning models (4{,}332 judged responses), models produce ungrounded code on about 60\% of unsatisfiable prompts and refuse only 27\%, while wrongly refusing 0\% of the solvable controls.

</details>


### [6] [TIPCODER: Reinforcement Learning Boosted Test-time Instruction Proposer for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03309)
*Minyu Chen,Sihao Wu,Ling-I Wu,Song Qin,Jingyang Li,Lei Ning,Jianxin Xue,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TipCoder通过在测试时生成问题特定的辅助提示，探索指令空间以提升代码生成质量，并结合奖励模型从基础解与提示引导解中选择更优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试时扩展主要对固定指令进行多次代码采样，难以解决原始提示中缺失约束、忽略边界条件或存在误导性推理路径等问题。因此，论文探索实例级指令空间，以主动修正和补充问题理解。

Method: TipCoder首先从多轮调试轨迹中蒸馏出主动式指导信息，训练一个测试时指令提议器；随后利用基于边际效用的奖励进行强化学习优化。推理阶段同时生成基础解和提示引导解，再通过奖励模型进行事后筛选，以扩大候选潜力并降低无效提示导致的性能退化。

Result: 在多个代码生成基准和目标代码大语言模型上，TipCoder表现出稳定的指令级测试时扩展能力。在统一的基于奖励模型的选择协议下，其整体效果优于随机采样和通用提示优化等基线，或具有更强竞争力。

Conclusion: 论文表明，测试时指令空间探索是代码生成中区别于单纯候选采样的有效扩展方向。通过问题特定提示、候选探索与奖励模型选择相结合，TipCoder能够更可靠地改善代码生成结果。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for code generation typically explores the solution space by sampling multiple programs from a fixed instruction. We study a complementary direction: instance-level instruction-space exploration. Our observation is that many coding failures stem from missing constraints, overlooked edge cases, or misleading reasoning paths induced by the original prompt. To address this, we propose TipCoder, a test-time instruction proposer that generates problem-specific auxiliary tips before code synthesis. TipCoder distills multi-turn debugging trajectories into proactive guidance and further optimizes the Proposer with reinforcement learning using a marginal-utility reward. At inference time, it generates both a base solution and a tip-guided solution, and applies a Reward Model for post-hoc selection. This exploration-selection design allows tips to expose additional candidate potential while reducing regressions from unnecessary guidance. Across the evaluated code-generation benchmarks and target Code LLMs, TipCoder provides a consistent instruction-level test-time scaling strategy, comparing favorably with stochastic sampling and generic prompt optimization baselines under a shared reward-model-based selection protocol.

</details>


### [7] [The Psychological Costs of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03456)
*Adam Alami,Elda Paja,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨软件组织引入生成式人工智能后，软件专业人员所承受的心理成本及其应对方式。基于一家大型软件开发服务公司的案例研究和21名参与者的访谈与会议数据，研究发现AI采用带来了责任焦虑、工艺身份受扰、意义感与满意度下降、认知和工作负荷强化以及不确定性压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组织AI采用策略主要关注生产率等可量化收益，却较少关注AI对角色认同、团队规范、工作意义和职业满意度的影响。研究旨在揭示软件工程领域AI采用过程中可能被忽视的心理与社会代价。

Method: 研究采用单案例质性研究方法，研究对象为一家大型软件开发服务公司在启动AI采用一年后的软件专业人员。数据通过会议观察和半结构化访谈收集，共涉及21名参与者，并据此分析AI采用引发的心理成本及其应对实践。

Result: 研究识别出五类主要心理成本：责任焦虑、工艺身份 disruption、工作意义与满意度侵蚀、认知负荷和工作负荷强化，以及不确定性压力。专业人员主要通过恢复控制、采取保护性和身份维护性适应措施来缓解这些成本；对于无法解决的部分，则选择承受和吸收。

Conclusion: AI在软件工程中的采用不应仅被视为技术升级或组织变革，也应被理解为涉及人的职业身份、工作意义和心理适应的人本转型。组织在追求生产率收益的同时，应系统评估并支持员工应对AI带来的心理成本。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to augment software engineering (SE) workflows. While code generation remains the main use case, organizations are actively seeking AI integration in other practices such as test cases generation and code reviews. Organizational AI adoption strategies seem to focus on tangible outcomes such as productivity. However, AI is a disruptive force, introduced into settings where role identity, team norms, and the sources of job satisfaction were well established before the recent advances in generative AI. Historically, technological disruptions have caused psychological and social strains in workplaces, ranging from anxiety and eroded meaning to deskilling and disrupted professional identities. The assumption that AI for SE is cost-free may not be accurate. Therefore, in this study we sought to understand the psychological costs software professionals experience during organizational AI adoption. We carried out a case study in a large software development services company, one year after the company launched its AI adoption. We collected qualitative data through meetings and semi-structured interviews (N = 21). We found that software professionals experience accountability anxiety, craft identity disruption, meaning and satisfaction erosion, cognitive and workload intensification, and uncertainty distress. Practitioners manage these costs through practices that restore control, mitigate them through protective and identity-preserving adaptations, or absorb them, carrying what neither can resolve. We contribute to AI-human collaboration in SE by repositioning AI adoption as a human transition, not only a technological and organizational one.

</details>


### [8] [Code Transformation Rule Synthesis using LLMs: Potential and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03592)
*Axel Allain,Aymeric Blot,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证评估了GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B为Comby、GritQL及Ast-Grep生成代码变换规则的能力，覆盖API误用修复、程序修复、API迁移和语言版本迁移四类任务。结果表明，前沿模型已具备较强的规则合成能力，但模型规模和任务复杂度显著影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM具有黑盒性、解释性有限和输出不确定等问题，在大型代码库的重复性软件演化任务中还可能产生较高使用成本。研究旨在评估LLM能否生成可解释、可复用、可泛化且正确的领域特定代码变换规则，并与传统抗统一算法进行比较。

Method: 研究针对Comby、GritQL和Ast-Grep三种变换规则DSL，测试GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B。实验使用六个数据集，覆盖API误用修复、程序修复、API迁移和语言版本迁移四类任务，并从规则适用率、与真实变换的接近程度、泛化能力、复用率和正确性等维度进行评估，同时将LLM方法与抗统一算法进行对比。

Result: GPT-5.4在多数基准上取得稳定较高的规则适用率，并生成最接近真实变换的规则。GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B在简单、局部化修改中仍然有效，但面对复杂迁移任务时表现较弱。元变量的使用带来了非忽略的泛化能力，多个数据集第一四分位数中也观察到较高的规则复用率。与抗统一算法相比，LLM在正确性上更优，但规则适用率较低。

Conclusion: LLM规则合成已从概念验证阶段向实际可行方向发展，尤其是强大的前沿模型能够生成较为正确、健壮、可泛化和可复用的变换规则。不过，复杂迁移场景中的适用率和小模型的稳定性仍是主要限制，未来需要进一步提升规则覆盖范围、确定性和应用可靠性。

Abstract: Due to their black-box nature, LLMs suffer from limited explain- ability and a lack of determinism. Their usage cost can also rise, particularly with repetitive tasks on large codebases. To mitigate this, we conduct a novel empirical study targeting three domain- specific languages for transformation rules, namely Comby, GritQL, and Ast-Grep. We evaluate three LLMs (GPT-5.4, GPT-oss-120B, and Llama3.1-8B) on six diverse datasets covering four software- evolution tasks: API misuse correction, program repair, API migra- tion, and language version migration. Our results provide evidence that transformation rule synthesis moves beyond proof-of-concept with strong frontier models. GPT-5.4 achieves consistently high rule applicability rates and produces transformations closest to the ground truth across most benchmarks. Smaller and open-weight GPT-oss-120B and Llama3.1-8B models remain effective for simpler, localized changes but struggle with complex migration scenarios. We also observe non-negligible generalizability through the usage of meta-variables and through a high reuse score in the first quartile of many datasets. Finally, when compared to the anti-unification algorithm, LLMs outperform it in correctness, but underperform in rule applicability. Overall, our results show great potential for LLMs to generate sound, correct, generalizable, and reusable rules.

</details>


### [9] [Can LLMs Extract Architectural Design Decisions from Source Code Commits? - A Preliminary Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03721)
*Amey Karan,Rudra Dhar,Mohamed Soliman,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文开展了一项初步研究，评估四种大语言模型从源代码提交中恢复架构设计决策（ADD）的能力。结果显示模型与人工ADD具有较高语义对齐度，但生成内容通常过长、偏重实现细节，且难以捕捉决策 rationale。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADD记录软件架构的结构、演化及其决策依据，是架构知识管理的重要基础。然而，ADD通常未被显式记录，而是隐含在提交信息和代码变更中，导致自动提取困难。尽管LLM具备代码与文本理解能力，其在ADD恢复任务中的有效性仍缺乏系统研究。

Method: 研究选取开源项目中开发者撰写的30条ADD，测试Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2和Qwen3四种LLM，并分别采用零样本和少样本提示。输出通过ROUGE-L、BLEU、METEOR和BERTScore进行评估，同时由一名作者对Gemini生成结果开展人工审查。

Result: 所有模型的BERT-F1均超过0.81，少样本提示能够提升结果与参考ADD的语义一致性；以Gemini为例，BERT-F1从0.828提升至0.847。定性分析表明，生成的ADD普遍篇幅过长、过度聚焦实现细节，并且经常遗漏架构决策背后的 rationale。

Conclusion: LLM能够较好地生成与参考ADD语义相关的内容，但当前方法尚不足以准确恢复完整的架构决策及其 rationale。未来应发展具备架构知识、能够区分决策内容与实现细节并强化 rationale 提取能力的LLM系统，以支持自动化架构知识管理。

Abstract: Context: Architectural Design Decisions (ADDs) capture the rationale behind the structure and evolution of software systems but are rarely documented explicitly, and are often hidden inside source code commits. Recovering them is important for Architectural Knowledge Management (AKM). Problem: Extracting ADDs from commits is challenging due to their implicit and unstructured nature. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in understanding code and text, yet their effectiveness for this task remains underexplored. Study: We present a preliminary study using four LLMs (Gemini 3 Pro, DeepSeek R1, Kimi K2, Qwen3) with zeroshot and fewshot prompting on 30 developer-written ADDs from open-source projects. We score outputs with ROUGE-L, BLEU, METEOR, and BERTScore, and one author manually reviews the Gemini outputs. Results: All models reach a BERT-F1 above 0.81, and fewshot prompting improves alignment (Gemini BERT-F1: 0.828 to 0.847). However, the generated ADDs are often too long, implementation-focused, and miss the rationale behind the decision. This highlights opportunities for architecture-aware LLM systems and automated AKM.

</details>


### [10] [No One Left Behind: Cross-Level Analysis for Sustainable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03861)
*Masoum Salehi,Sandro Schulze,Jacob Krüger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出“可持续性反模式”概念，用于解释软件工程中组织、过程与产品层面的相互作用如何系统性地产生并强化不可持续结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件可持续性研究主要聚焦能源效率、资源优化或代码可维护性等单一维度，难以解释不同社会技术层级之间的错位，以及局部改进为何可能引发长期的跨层级负面影响。

Method: 作为一篇愿景论文，作者将可持续性问题视为系统性现象，提出以可持续性反模式描述会持续导致不可持续结果的条件，并展示该概念如何揭示社会技术层级与可持续性维度之间的跨层级错配；同时提出识别、形式化、检测和缓解反模式的研究方向。

Result: 论文表明，可持续性反模式能够补充以软件制品或单一可持续性维度为中心的分析，帮助发现组织优先级、开发实践与技术条件之间难以识别的相互作用及其累积影响。

Conclusion: 可持续性反模式可作为构建综合且可执行的软件工程可持续性研究议程的基础，为系统化理解、检测和治理跨层级可持续性问题提供统一框架。

Abstract: Software engineers expect a software system to be efficient, maintainable, economically viable, and socially responsible throughout its lifecycle. Unfortunately, decisions made at the organizational, process, or product levels of software development - even when they improve one of these goals - often have unintended long-term consequences at other levels. In response, software-engineering research has explored different ways to achieve software sustainability, including energy efficiency, resource optimization, and code maintainability. However, existing research offers limited explanations for how sustainability problems emerge and reinforce one another across the socio-technical levels of software engineering. Moreover, we argue that sustainability challenges are not isolated but rather systemic: They stem from interactions among organizational priorities, development practices, and technical conditions. In this vision paper, we introduce the concept of sustainability antipatterns to capture conditions that systematically produce unsustainable outcomes. We demonstrate how this concept can help expose cross-level misalignments between socio-technical levels and sustainability dimensions that remain difficult to recognize through artifact-centric or dimension-specific analyses. To guide future research, we outline concrete directions for identifying, formalizing, detecting, and mitigating sustainability anti-patterns. We envision sustainability anti-patterns as a foundation for an integrated and actionable research agenda on sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [11] [LabelMate: An LLM-Driven Framework for Refined Issue Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04055)
*Liam Johnston,Shayan Noei,Maram Assi,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LabelMate 是一种由大语言模型驱动的自动化问题报告标注框架，可从历史报告中生成项目特定的标签体系，并在无需预标注训练数据的情况下为新报告分配标签。在涵盖30个GitHub仓库的16,500条问题报告上，生成275个标签，平均标注准确率达到89.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题报告标注方法通常需要大量人工设计标签体系和标注数据，容易产生通用性较强但缺乏项目针对性的标签，并且依赖已有的人工标注数据，导致实施成本高、适应性有限。

Method: LabelMate利用大语言模型分析历史问题报告，自动归纳出全面且符合项目语境的标签集合；随后基于该标签集合，为新的问题报告自动分配相关标签，全流程不依赖预先标注的训练数据。研究在30个具有多样性的GitHub开源仓库、共16,500条问题报告上进行评估。

Result: 框架从数据中生成了275个具有一致性的标签，平均标注准确率为89.84%。与现有的通用标签分配方法相比，LabelMate取得了统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: LabelMate能够以较少人工干预实现项目自适应的问题报告标注，为缺乏预标注数据的场景提供了一种高效、可扩展的自动化解决方案，有助于改进问题管理、规划和社区协作。

Abstract: Software users often submit issue reports to a product's issue tracking system to report defects, suggest enhancements, or raise other product-related concerns. Labeling these issue reports supports effective planning and improves community engagement. However, many issue reports remain unlabeled due to the substantial manual effort required to design an appropriate label taxonomy, then assign suitable labels from this taxonomy to new issue reports. Existing automated labeling approaches attempt to mitigate these challenges. However, they suffer from key limitations, such as extensive manual intervention, the assignment of generic labels, and a dependence on existing labeled datasets. To address these limitations, we propose LabelMate, a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven framework that (1) derives a comprehensive, project-specific label set from historical issue reports and (2) automatically assigns relevant labels to new issue reports without requiring any pre-labeled training data. We evaluate LabelMate on 16,500 issue reports from 30 popular and diverse GitHub repositories. Based on this dataset, our approach generates a coherent list of 275 labels and achieves an average labeling accuracy of 89.84%, a statistically significant improvement over existing generic label assigning approaches. These results demonstrate that LabelMate offers an efficient, domain-adaptive solution to streamline the issue labeling process.

</details>


### [12] [When Models Edit Too Much: On the Fidelity of Minimal Code Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04061)
*Tongyao Zhu,Wei Hern Lim,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在代码修复中的“过度编辑”问题，即修复缺陷时改动超出必要范围。基于400个BigCodeBench问题和受控AST级扰动，研究发现过度编辑普遍存在；加入保持原实现的指令、以及针对编辑保真度的强化学习，能够有效减少非必要改动并提升修复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码修复的质量不仅取决于是否通过测试，还应具备改动最小、易于审查和忠实保留原实现等特征。现有评估主要关注正确性，难以衡量模型是否进行了不必要的重写，因此需要建立可量化的编辑保真度评估框架，并研究其可学习性。

Method: 研究者从400个BigCodeBench问题的参考解答出发，注入受控的AST级代码错误，使每个任务具有已知的最小修复补丁。随后评估多个前沿大语言模型的修复结果，并使用超额Levenshtein距离、认知复杂度变化和Pass@1衡量过度编辑及修复性能。此外，研究比较了保持原实现指令、不同模型规模与推理预算，以及监督微调和强化学习对编辑保真度的影响。

Result: 过度编辑在不同模型中普遍存在，甚至出现在能力较强的模型中；高Pass@1并不意味着改动足够精简。加入保持原实现的指令后，平均超额Levenshtein距离从0.195降至0.131，新增认知复杂度降低26.6%，Pass@1提高2.3个百分点。上述改进并非简单源于更大的模型或推理预算。监督微调容易对已见的错误模式过拟合，而强化学习在域外编辑保真度与性能保持之间取得了最佳权衡。

Conclusion: 编辑保真度应被视为代码修复质量中独立于正确性的关键维度。该指标可以通过最小补丁和结构化扰动进行测量，也能够通过适当的提示和后训练方法加以优化，其中强化学习比监督微调更有利于获得稳健的域外最小编辑能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to edit existing code, but correctness alone is not enough: useful repairs should also be minimal, reviewable, and faithful to the original implementation. We study over-editing, the tendency of a model to rewrite code beyond what is required to fix a bug. We construct an evaluation framework from 400 BigCodeBench problems by injecting controlled AST-level corruptions into reference solutions, giving each repair task a known minimal patch. Across frontier LLMs, over-editing is widespread even among strong models like GPT-5.5: high Pass@1 can coexist with unnecessarily large edits and added cognitive complexity. A preservation instruction substantially reduces this behavior, lowering average excess Levenshtein distance from 0.195 to 0.131, reducing added cognitive complexity by 26.6%, and increasing Pass@1 by 2.3 points. However, these gains do not simply follow from a larger reasoning budget or larger models. We next ask whether minimal editing can be learned directly during post-training. We observe that supervised fine-tuning overfits to seen corruption patterns, whereas reinforcement learning gives the best out-of-domain edit-fidelity and performance-retention trade-off. These results position edit fidelity as a distinct axis of code-repair quality and show that it can be measured and learned.

</details>


### [13] [ATIBA: Grounded Integrity and Quality Checking for Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04123)
*Veli Karakaya,Semih Çağlar,Yusuf Yiğit Korkmaz,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATIBA是一款面向学术投稿的完整性与质量检查工具，集成参考文献核验、投稿要求合规性检查、经验研究规范检查、多模式AI评审和引文推荐五项功能。其核心原则是：大语言模型只能依据可验证证据进行判断，不能自行生成证据。13名非作者参与的用户研究显示，用户对其有用性的平均认可度为85%，但客观准确性尚未验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稿件的参考文献完整性、目标期刊或会议的投稿规则以及社区报告规范通常依赖人工检查，过程重复、耗时且因 venue 不同而难以标准化，实际执行中容易不一致或被跳过。因此，需要一种能够自动化检查并提供可追溯证据的工具。

Method: ATIBA实施五类检查：1）从书目来源核验每条引文，并标记撤回或无法找到的参考文献；2）直接读取 venue 的征稿页面，提取投稿标准并逐项评估，且每个结论均关联页面中的逐字引文；3）依据 ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards 检查经验研究规范，并删除无法在稿件中逐字定位的证据；4）通过 Azure OpenAI 调用 GPT-5.4，提供 venue 特定、正式评审和页面锚定批注三种AI评审模式；5）推荐潜在参考文献，并在展示前逐一通过书目来源核验。研究采用包含13名非作者参与者的调节式用户研究，以六项问卷指标评估感知有用性。

Result: 六项问卷指标的一致认可度介于69%至92%之间，平均为85%，表明参与者对ATIBA各项工作流具有积极的感知有用性。研究验证了用户层面的初步接受度，但没有测量工具的客观准确率。

Conclusion: ATIBA通过证据锚定和可验证引用增强了学术稿件自动检查的可靠性，展示了整合多类投稿质量控制任务的可行性。当前结论仅支持其感知有用性，未来仍需通过基准数据、人工专家对照和错误分析系统评估其实际准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Checking a manuscript's reference integrity, its compliance with a target venue's specific submission rules, and its adherence to community reporting standards is manual, repetitive, and different for every venue so in practice it is done inconsistently or skipped. We present ATIBA, a tool that runs five grounded integrity and quality checks on a manuscript: a reference-integrity check that verifies each citation against bibliographic sources and flags retracted or unfindable references; a venue/track compliance check that derives submission criteria directly from a venue's own call-for-papers page and evaluates the manuscript against them, each verdict anchored to a verbatim quote from that page; an empirical-standards compliance check against the ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards, with a hallucination defence that discards any evidence quote it cannot locate verbatim in the manuscript; a multi-mode AI review (venue-specific, formal, and page-anchored annotation) powered by GPT-5.4 through Azure OpenAI; and a citation-suggestion feature that proposes candidate references for a manuscript and verifies each against bibliographic sources before it is shown to the user. All five checks are designed around the same principle: an LLM is only trusted to judge, never to invent the evidence it judges against. We evaluated ATIBA through a moderated user study with 13 non-author participants. Agreement across the six survey items ranged from 69% to 92%, with a mean of 85%, providing initial evidence of positive perceived usefulness across the evaluated workflows. These findings establish perceived usefulness; objective accuracy remains to be measured.

</details>


### [14] [SWE-Gate: Passing Functional Tests Is Not Enough for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04167)
*Xin He,Yanlin Wang,Mingwei Liu,Jiachi Chen,Hongyu Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Gate是一个面向仓库级软件工程智能体的基准，除功能正确性外，还显式评估其对代码审查约束的遵循能力。实验表明，仅通过功能测试会高估智能体完成真实修复任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级软件工程基准主要关注生成补丁能否通过功能测试，却忽略了源自代码审查意见的接受约束。这些约束通常决定补丁能否在真实开发流程中被接受，因此有必要将功能正确性与审查约束合并评估。

Method: 研究者从真实拉取请求的审查评论中提取审查约束，并围绕这些约束构造仓库级修复实例。每个实例包含独立的功能测试和约束测试，以及不合规补丁和金标准补丁，从而区分问题解决能力与审查约束遵循能力。基准包含来自75个开源Python仓库的303个修复实例，并在统一编码智能体框架下评估了四种能力层次不同的大语言模型后端。

Result: 在通过功能测试的644次修复中，有221次未满足给定的审查约束，约占34.3%。结果显示，智能体在功能测试上的成功并不代表其满足完整修复规范，功能正确性与审查约束合规性之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: SWE-Gate补充了传统功能导向基准的评估缺口，证明仓库级软件工程智能体需要同时满足功能要求和代码审查约束。仅依赖功能测试会系统性高估智能体在真实软件开发任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering benchmarks have significantly advanced the evaluation of coding agents, but existing benchmarks primarily measure whether generated patches pass functional tests and overlook review-derived acceptance constraints (review constraints) that often influence whether a patch is acceptable in real-world software development. We introduce SWE-Gate, a repository-level benchmark for software engineering agents that explicitly evaluates review constraint compliance alongside functional correctness. SWE-Gate derives review constraints from real pull request review comments and synthesizes repository-level repair instances around these constraints. Each instance provides separate functional and constraint tests, together with non-compliant and gold patches, enabling explicit separation between issue resolution capability and review constraint compliance. We construct SWE-Gate with 303 repository-level repair instances spanning 75 open-source Python repositories across diverse software domains. Experiments with four LLM backends spanning different capability levels under a common coding-agent scaffold reveal a substantial gap between functional success and success under the complete repair specification: among 644 repairs that pass the functional tests, 221 fail to satisfy the provided review constraints. These findings show that functional-only evaluation overestimates agents' ability to satisfy the full requirements of repository-level repair tasks. The replication package including code, data, and experimental results is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/SWE-Gate.

</details>


### [15] [ExecRetrieval: Measuring the Functional-Correctness Gap in Code-Embedding Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01865)
*Aaryan Kapoor,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出ExecRetrieval基准，用执行验证过的单编辑错误代码作为与正确实现高度相似的干扰项，检验代码嵌入检索是否能按功能正确性而非表面相似性排序。结果显示，主流检索系统虽能在前10名中找到正确代码，但首位准确率较低，且首位误检几乎总是近克隆错误变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准通常缺少与标准正确实现仅有微小差异、但功能错误的受控候选代码。因此，已有评测难以区分模型究竟是在检索语义/功能正确的代码，还是仅依赖词法、主题或实现身份相似度。论文旨在构建包含功能反事实样本的检索池，直接测试模型能否辨别正确实现与近似但错误的代码。

Method: 作者构建ExecRetrieval：包含939个Python任务，每个任务配有一个经执行验证的标准实现，以及最多4个经执行验证的错误干扰实现。错误版本通过机械化的单点定向编辑生成，确保其与标准实现高度相似但存在功能缺陷。研究评估了23种稠密嵌入配置和BM25，并采用服务商原生调用方式；统计分析使用配对McNemar检验和查询级自助法置信区间。

Result: 在将近克隆错误变体置入检索池后，表现最好的托管系统达到exec@10=1.00，但exec@1仅为0.331，说明正确代码通常能被召回，却常无法排在首位。四个领先系统的首位错误中，91.5%至99.4%是与正确实现配对的错误变体；对于领先系统，67%至78%的查询中，正确实现的得分低于至少一个对应错误干扰项。这表明当前嵌入排序对细粒度功能正确性的区分能力明显不足。

Conclusion: ExecRetrieval揭示了代码嵌入检索的重要失效模式：高召回不等于可靠的首位功能正确性排序。现有系统在面对单编辑、执行可验证的近克隆错误代码时，主要依赖相似性而非功能判别。该基准及其执行预言机、嵌入矩阵、环境快照和统计检验的公开，可为后续开发面向功能正确性的代码检索器与评测方法提供基础。

Abstract: Embedding-based code retrieval is a core component of coding agents and retrieval-augmented code generation, where retrieving correct code matters more than retrieving lexically similar code. Existing code-retrieval benchmarks do not plant controlled, execution-verified single-edit variants of each query's canonical implementation in the search pool, leaving the question of whether embeddings can functionally discriminate correct from near-clone-but-incorrect code unanswered in a retrieval setting. Resolving this requires a benchmark whose search pool itself contains the relevant counterfactuals -- execution-verified buggy variants near-identical to each canonical -- so that a retriever's rank ordering can be directly tested for functional discrimination rather than topical or identity overlap. We introduce ExecRetrieval, 939 Python tasks each paired with one execution-verified canonical implementation and up to four execution-verified buggy distractors, each generated by a mechanical mutation making a single targeted edit, and evaluate 23 dense embedding configurations plus BM25 under provider-native invocation with paired McNemar tests and query-level bootstrap intervals. With near-clone counterfactuals in the pool, the top hosted system reaches exec@10 = 1.00 but only exec@1 = 0.331; rank-1 misses are paired buggy variants 91.5-99.4% of the time across the four leading systems, and the canonical scores below at least one of its four paired distractors in 67-78% of queries on the leading systems. The full dataset, execution oracle, embedding matrices, environment snapshot, and pairwise statistical tests are released at the URL in Appendix D.

</details>

### [16] [Enhancing the Power of Polyhedral-Based Optimizations with Coordinate-Based Hill Climbing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03114)
*Gaurav Verma,Michael Canesche,Fernando Magno Quintão Pereira*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出在多面体编译器 Pluto 之后加入轻量级、逐坐标爬山搜索调优器，用于自动优化分块大小、线程块维度等数值变换参数；该方法以较低搜索成本获得接近 AutoTVM 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pluto 能自动确定循环变换结构，但默认数值参数通常无法适应具体硬件与程序特征。完整自动调优搜索成本较高，而固定代价模型又可能产生次优参数，因此需要一种兼顾编译开销与性能收益的后优化调参方案。

Method: 在 Pluto 完成内核循环结构选择后，对数值参数执行逐坐标 hill-climbing 搜索。为改善普通爬山法容易陷入局部最优且收敛范围有限的问题，方法加入扩展邻域探索，并使用最短跳数细化阶段进一步定位更优参数。该框架应用于 CPU 上的分块参数以及 GPU 上的线程块分配参数。

Result: 在 11 个基准测试中，调优后的 x86 和 ARM CPU 内核相对 Pluto 默认配置取得 1.06 至 1.28 倍的几何平均加速，并优于 Clang -O3、Polly 和 IOOpt 等静态优化方案。其性能可与 AutoTVM 自动调优器竞争，但搜索成本更低。在 NVIDIA A100 上，GPU 线程块配置调优相对默认配置提升 5.5% 至 8.5%。

Conclusion: 后优化阶段的轻量级参数调优能够有效补足多面体编译器固定参数选择的不足，在性能、实现复杂度和搜索成本之间提供实用折中。该工作表明，先确定循环结构、再针对硬件执行局部数值参数搜索，是实现高效编译优化的可行路径。

Abstract: This paper describes our experience extending the polyhedral compiler Pluto with a lightweight, coordinate-wise hill-climbing tuner that adjusts numeric transformation parameters, such as tile sizes and thread-block dimensions, after Pluto selects the kernel's loop structure. To ensure fast convergence and escape local minima, hill climbing is augmented with two techniques: expanded neighborhood exploration and a shortest-hop refinement phase. On x86 and ARM CPUs, tuned kernels outperform Pluto's default configuration (1.06-1.28x geometric mean speedup across 11 benchmarks) and static optimizers (Clang -O3, Polly, IOOpt), reaching performance competitive with the AutoTVM autotuner at substantially lower search cost. Applying the same technique to GPU thread-block allocation on an NVIDIA A100 yields 5.5-8.5% improvement over default configurations. These results position post-optimization parameter tuning as a practical middle ground between fixed-cost-model polyhedral compilation and full autotuning.

</details>


### [17] [Typed Flexible-Arity Slotted E-Graphs: A Soundness Construction and an Alloy Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03998)
*Guanxuan Wu,Allison Sullivan*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种将槽位化 e-graph 与代数算子的序列、袋和集合式子节点规范表示相结合的规格级框架，并通过有限商表示、最小轨道范式及认证记录证明其形式性质；Alloy 案例研究仅评估有界能力与结构整合，未验证实现细化或实验回放一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放项需要在一致重命名下进行等价处理，适合由 slotted e-graph 表达；而代数算子通常要求对子节点采用序列、重集或集合等规范化表示。现有方法若分别处理这两类需求，难以保证组合后的等价关系、扁平化规则和操作端口约束具有可证明的正确性，因此需要统一的形式化规格。

Method: 方法在规格层组合两类结构：以类型化、槽位映射的调用表示操作符端口；分别认证兄弟节点的商关系与递归扁平化许可。论文使用通用有限商表示证明最小轨道范式的精确性，并利用认证记录定义有效支持集上的核提取与碰撞处理。对于携带局部端点证书的抽象义务轨迹，证明有限展开下的等式健全性。最后通过 Alloy 对七个相关流水线分支、固定语料库和受控变换集合进行案例分析。

Result: 理论结果包括：有限商框架下最小轨道正规形式与目标等价关系精确对应；认证数据结构能够支持有效支持集核提取和冲突处理；含局部端点证书的义务轨迹在有限展开条件下满足等式健全性。案例研究表明该框架可刻画不同流水线分支的有界能力及结构整合情况。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献在于为开放项重命名等价与代数子节点规范化的组合提供了可认证、可证明的规格框架。其证据主要覆盖形式模型及 Alloy 中的有界分析；论文明确不声称 Java 实现已被证明细化该模型，也未证明实验结果可相对于形式模型重放，因此工程实现正确性和端到端验证仍是后续工作。

Abstract: Slotted e-graphs represent open terms modulo consistent renaming, while algebraic operators benefit from canonical sequence, bag, or set children. We compose the two at a specification level: typed slot-mapped invocations inhabit operator-declared ports whose sibling quotient and recursive flattening licenses are certified separately. A generic finite quotient presentation proves exactness of its least-orbit normal form, while certified records specify effective-support kernel extraction and collision. For abstract obligation traces carrying local endpoint certificates, we prove finite-unfolding equational soundness. An Alloy case study compares seven related pipeline arms on a frozen corpus and a controlled transformation suite. Its measurements characterize bounded capability and structural consolidation; they do not establish refinement of the Java artifact or experimental replay against the formal model.

</details>

### [18] [Target Discounted Sum Problem on Markov Chains with Applications to Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03670)
*Nathalie Bertrand,Pranav Ghorpade,Senthil Rajasekaran,Sasha Rubin,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究带折扣求和目标的马尔可夫链与马尔可夫决策过程，证明满足精确目标值且具有无限多个不同后缀和的路径事件概率为零，并据此给出可计算性与策略结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典目标折扣和问题询问是否存在一个无限字母序列，使其折扣和恰好等于给定目标值；该问题目前仍未解决。本文转而研究其概率版本，以刻画随机系统中精确达到目标的可行性，并进一步处理带决策的系统。

Method: 首先分析马尔可夫链路径折扣和的结构，证明同时满足目标值且具有无限多个不同后缀和的路径集合概率为零。利用这一结构性质，采用自动机理论方法判定马尔可夫链达到目标折扣和的概率性质。随后将技术结果推广到带目标折扣和目标的马尔可夫决策过程，并分析策略的记忆需求与最优值。

Result: 马尔可夫链中的特殊路径事件被证明具有概率零，从而目标折扣和问题可以通过自动机理论方法求解。对于马尔可夫决策过程，最小值与有限记忆最大值均可在伪多项式时间内计算，并且二者都能由确定性有限记忆策略达到。

Conclusion: 本文建立了目标折扣和问题在随机系统中的结构化求解框架，并证明了其在马尔可夫链和马尔可夫决策过程中的可计算性。结果表明，尽管一般序列情形仍然开放，随机与决策模型下可以获得有效算法及确定性有限记忆最优策略。

Abstract: The discounted sum is a way to aggregate a sequence of weights from a finite alphabet $Σ$, i.e., for a discount factor $λ$, the discounted sum of a sequence $w_0 w_1 w_2 \cdots$ over $Σ$ is $\sum_{i \in \mathbb{N}} w_i λ^i$. The target discounted-sum problem, which is currently open, asks, given $λ,Σ$ and a target $t$, whether there exists an infinite sequence over $Σ$ whose discounted sum is equal to $t$. We study and solve a probabilistic variant of this problem, i.e., the target discounted-sum problem on Markov chains. To do this, we prove that the event consisting of paths whose discounted sum is equal to the target and has infinitely many distinct suffix sums has probability zero. This structural property allows us to solve the target discounted-sum problem on Markov chains using an automata-theoretic technique. We apply our technical results to Markov decision processes with target discounted-sum objectives: we show that the infimum value and the finite-memory supremum value are computable in pseudo-polynomial time and are attained by deterministic finite-memory strategies.

</details>


### [19] [Extending concurrent separation logic to the hardware level to verify the xv6 OS kernel on RISC-V with AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04043)
*M. Frans Kaashoek,Nickolai Zeldovich*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出MachCSL：一个基于Iris并适配Sail RISC-V语义的并发分离逻辑验证框架，用于在子指令级硬件语义上验证操作系统内核等系统软件。作者以6,593行C和汇编实现的xv6为案例，在77天内完成框架开发与验证，并发现xv6中的9个缺陷及Sail RISC-V语义中的1个缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统软件验证通常难以精确覆盖页表转换、TLB、特权级、寄存器配置、取指译码执行、异常中断、DMA、共享内存和断电等底层硬件细节；而这些细节直接影响内核正确性。论文旨在建立一种能够对软硬件交互进行细粒度、并发且可机助推理的验证方法。

Method: MachCSL将基于Iris的并发分离逻辑扩展到RISC-V机器的低层执行模型，以Sail RISC-V语义作为硬件行为基础，并在子指令粒度描述和证明软件对硬件状态的管理正确性。论文使用具备低层推理能力的LLM代理辅助验证，将该方法应用于xv6的多核、细粒度锁、共享内存、中断、DMA及抢占式调度等并发机制。

Result: 案例验证覆盖xv6提供的传统Unix接口及其关键内部并发机制。验证过程中发现9个xv6实现缺陷，说明该框架能够揭示实际内核中的错误；同时发现1个Sail RISC-V语义缺陷，表明方法还可用于审查硬件形式化模型本身。总体工作耗时77天，且包含MachCSL框架的开发时间。

Conclusion: MachCSL表明，基于精确RISC-V语义、在子指令粒度进行并发分离逻辑验证，能够实现对真实操作系统内核的端到端低层正确性分析。其主要贡献在于将硬件微观执行细节纳入系统软件验证，并通过xv6案例证明了其实用性和缺陷发现能力；但摘要未给出证明规模、自动化程度、LLM代理的具体作用及与既有验证框架的定量比较，这些仍需结合全文评估。

Abstract: MachCSL is a framework for verifying systems software, such as an OS kernel, on top of low-level semantics of a RISC-V computer, based on the Sail RISC-V semantics. The key idea behind MachCSL is to adapt concurrent separation logic, based on Iris, to reasoning about low-level hardware execution at the sub-instruction level: page-table translation, TLB, privilege levels, configuration registers, instruction fetch/decode/execute, traps and interrupts, DMA, shared memory, power failures, etc. Reasoning at this level of detail ensures that the system software correctly manages all of the hardware details.
  Verifying software at this low level of abstraction is tedious, but LLM-based agents are capable of reasoning about such low-level details. As a case study, we verify the xv6 OS kernel (6,593 lines of C and assembly code), which provides a traditional Unix system call interface (processes, file system, file descriptors, and preemptive scheduling) and has substantial internal concurrency (multi-core support with fine-grained locking, shared memory, interrupts, DMA, etc.). In the verification process, we uncovered nine bugs in the xv6 implementation, as well as one bug in the Sail RISC-V semantics. The verification effort took us 77 days, including the time to develop the MachCSL framework.

</details>
