### [89] [A Public-Key-Dependent Adversarial-Deletion Ceiling for Fixed-Alphabet Multi-Bit Pseudorandom Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02943)
*Frederick Dehmel,Shilun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明：针对可依赖公钥和传输码字的自适应删除信道，固定字母表上的多消息公钥伪随机码（PRC）无法在删除率超过1-γ_q^{LCS}时实现单输出可靠解码；对二元字母表，该阈值低于0.207334008。有限列表解码时，阈值同样受多序列最长公共子序列长度限制，并趋近于1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究公钥伪随机码在强自适应删除攻击下的可靠性极限，明确伪随机性、公共编码密钥和删除信道依赖码字之间的关系，并刻画单输出与有限列表解码的不可行区域。

Method: 利用独立均匀q元字符串之间的最长公共子序列（LCS）事件，并通过伪随机性将该事件转移到独立采样的码字上。随后构造基于碰撞的删除攻击，证明即使不使用秘密密钥，攻击者也能造成解码歧义。该论证本质上是信息论的；列表解码情形则扩展到L+1个消息，并使用多序列LCS常数γ_2^{(L+1)}。

Result: 对任意固定q≥2，当删除率δ>1-γ_q^{LCS}时，不存在具有单输出解码器、可支持多个消息的公钥PRC。对于q=2，已知γ_2≥0.792665992，因此所有δ>0.207334008均被排除。若列表大小至多为固定常数L且消息数至少为L+1，则当δ>1-γ_2^{(L+1)}时也不存在鲁棒PRC；由于γ_2^{(m)}=1/2+Θ(1/√m)，相应阈值趋近于1/2。

Conclusion: 本文给出了公钥依赖型自适应删除信道下PRC的无条件不可行性界，表明LCS结构会限制高删除率下的唯一或有限列表解码能力。该结论依赖信道对公钥和码字的适应性，不适用于oblivious（与公钥和码字无关的）编辑信道。

Abstract: A pseudorandom code (PRC) is a keyed error-correcting code whose codewords are computationally indistinguishable from uniform strings. We study public-key PRCs over fixed alphabets against adversarial deletions, where the deletion channel may both depend on the public encoding key and the transmitted codeword.
  Let $γ_q^{\mathrm{LCS}}$ denote the asymptotic normalised longest-common-subsequence length of two independent uniform $q$-ary strings. We prove that for every fixed $q\ge2$ no multi-message public-key PRC with a single-output decoder is robust against all such $δ$-deletion channels for any $δ>1-γ_q^{\mathrm{LCS}}$. For $q=2$, the current rigorous bound $γ_2\ge0.792665992$ rules out every constant $δ>0.207334008$. The proof uses pseudorandomness only to transfer an LCS event from uniform strings to independently sampled codewords and therefore also the resulting collision argument is information-theoretic and requires no secret key. We also extend the argument to list decoding: for every fixed constant $L$, provided the message space contains at least $L+1$ messages, no such PRC with output lists of size at most $L$ is robust for $δ>1-γ_2^{(L+1)}$. Since $γ_2^{(m)}=1/2+Θ(1/\sqrt m)$, these thresholds approach $1/2$. Our bounds are specific to public-key-dependent adversarial channels and do not apply to oblivious edit channels.

</details>


### [90] [Privacy-Preserving Heterogeneous Multi-LLM Federated Inference for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02947)
*Yagna Manasa Boyapati,Chong Yu,Tianyu Jiang,Justin Zhan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向认知诊断的联邦多大语言模型推理框架：多个商业LLM API在不共享学生原始数据和模型内部信息的条件下协作，并通过本地差分隐私与残差聚合兼顾隐私保护和诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育系统中的认知诊断需要利用学生学习数据，但原始数据共享会带来隐私风险；同时，不同商业LLM API具有异构能力与封闭模型结构，难以直接联合训练或统一推理。因此，研究旨在构建无需暴露数据和模型参数的跨API协作机制。

Method: 框架采用异构多LLM联邦推理架构，将多个模型实体的预测结果作为协作基础。各实体先在本地预测输出上加入拉普拉斯噪声，以实现ε-本地差分隐私；随后对扰动后的输出进行聚合。为降低不同模型预测偏差和能力差异造成的影响，方法引入基于残差的聚合策略。威胁模型假设API提供方为诚实但好奇，即其会遵循协议但可能试图从可见信息中推断额外内容。

Result: 摘要声称理论隐私—效用分析表明，该机制能够提供较强的隐私保障，同时仅造成较小的准确率损失；在三个真实教育基准上的实验显示，该方法具有实际可用性，并具备一定跨领域泛化能力。摘要未给出具体的ε取值、准确率变化、基准数据集名称、聚合公式或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将本地差分隐私、联邦推理和异构LLM集成应用于教育认知诊断，为调用封闭商业API时的数据保护提供了可行路径。其主要贡献在于避免共享原始学生数据与模型内部信息，并通过残差聚合应对模型异构性。仍需进一步验证的关键点包括：本地扰动对诊断细粒度和校准性的影响、API查询本身可能泄露的信息、诚实但好奇假设的现实性，以及与安全聚合、客户端级差分隐私等替代方案的比较。

Abstract: Significant challenges remain in AI-driven educational systems in balancing privacy preservation with accurate cognitive diagnosis. To overcome this, we propose a federated inference framework in which several commercial LLM APIs collaborate without requiring access to raw student data or proprietary model internals. Using multiple federated entities, such as LLaMA-3.3-70B, GPT-4o-mini, and Claude-3-Haiku, our framework builds upon a heterogeneous multi-LLM architecture. The predictions generated by these entities are combined with epsilon-local differential privacy by adding Laplace noise locally to each entity's prediction output before aggregation, while residual-based aggregation mitigates model heterogeneity. Our approach is predicated on an honest-but-curious trust paradigm in which API providers are presumed not to abuse submitted queries, and our differential privacy mechanism shields the published diagnostic results from external inference. We conduct rigorous privacy-utility analysis showing strong privacy guarantees with minimal accuracy loss, and extensive real-world evaluations across three educational benchmarks confirm the framework's practical usability and cross-domain generalizability.

</details>


### [91] [PrivateHub: Contrastive Diffusion Model for Private Sensor-Intensive Environment Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02958)
*Jiechao Gao,Yuandong Pan,Jie Wang,Michael Lepech,Bradford Campbell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Privatehub是一种面向多传感器数据共享的隐私保护生成框架，通过扩散模型与对比学习生成合成传感器流，在保持非隐私应用可检测性的同时降低私密应用被推断的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器数据之间存在跨传感器推断风险，传统差分隐私或基于规则的单流过滤难以防止攻击者融合异构数据推断用户私密活动。因此，需要同时满足服务可用性与应用级隐私保护。

Method: Privatehub包含两个阶段：应用条件预训练（ACP）利用应用嵌入对多传感器数据进行条件建模；应用感知微调（AAF）通过对比学习区分私密应用与非私密应用，并引导扩散模型生成具有选择性可推断性的合成多传感器流。论文同时建立了多传感器共享场景下的威胁模型。

Result: 在三个真实世界多传感器数据集上的实验表明，Privatehub可将私密应用的识别准确率降低约40%至50%，同时基本不损害非隐私应用的识别性能；即使攻击者使用合成数据重新训练模型，该方法仍保持较强的隐私保护效果。

Conclusion: Privatehub有效解决了跨传感器应用推断带来的隐私风险，在保护私密应用的同时维持非私密应用的服务价值，证明了基于应用感知生成建模的多传感器隐私保护可行性。

Abstract: Sensor-intensive environments enable many intelligent services by inferring user applications from heterogeneous data streams. However, not all applications should be exposed: users want some activities to stay private. This creates a tension between inferring applications for useful services and preventing unwanted inference. Existing approaches such as differential privacy and rule-based filtering protect individual streams but cannot address the privacy risk from cross-sensor inference.
  We introduce Privatehub, which uses contrastive learning within a diffusion model to generate synthetic multi-sensor streams that keep non-private applications detectable while concealing private ones. Privatehub has two stages: App-Conditioned Pre-training (ACP), which conditions the model on multi-sensor data with application embeddings, and App-Aware Fine-tuning (AAF), which separates private from non-private data via contrastive learning. We also define a threat model for the multi-sensor sharing setting. Experiments on three real-world multi-sensor datasets show Privatehub lowers private-application accuracy by 40 to 50\% without hurting non-private performance, and stays robust when the attacker retrains on the synthetic data.

</details>


### [92] [When Optimization Becomes Manipulation: Defending Generative Search against Malicious Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02964)
*Haozhang Li,Yangguang Shao,Xinjie Lin,Zhong Guan,Mi Zhou,Junzheng Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出GEO Defender，用于防御针对生成式搜索引擎的恶意GEO文档改写攻击。该方法通过重排序阶段抑制攻击文档，并在生成阶段引导模型谨慎使用来源，在无需微调目标大模型的条件下显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化与智能体化GEO能够通过重写网页以迎合引擎引用偏好，从而提升目标文档在生成答案中的曝光与影响。现有防御面临两项困难：攻击文本通常仍与原文事实一致，因而难以被事实核验或困惑度检测识别；攻击强化的特征也可能是优质正常内容所具备的特征，导致简单过滤容易误伤正常证据。

Method: GEO Defender采用与攻击链对应的两阶段方案。第一阶段Shield Reranker在冻结的基础重排序器上学习基于偏好的防御残差，在保持相关性判断的同时降低GEO改写文档的排序。第二阶段Training-Free Shield Generation（TFSG）将防御结果蒸馏为自然语言“经验库”，在推理时指导目标大模型进行来源选择与利用。该框架不要求对目标大模型进行微调。

Result: 在2个闭源大模型、3个开源大模型及7种GEO攻击上的实验显示，GEO Defender将平均攻击成功率从50.32%降至6.20%；同时保留94.12%的正常证据使用率，维持答案质量，并能够从已知构造实例泛化至未见攻击。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，针对生成式搜索操纵的有效防御不应仅依赖内容真实性或表面文本异常检测，而应联合控制检索重排序和生成阶段的证据使用。GEO Defender通过轻量、无需微调目标模型的两阶段机制，在攻击抑制、正常证据保留和答案质量之间取得了较强平衡。

Abstract: This paper focuses on defending generative search engines against malicious Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), which rewrites web documents to match engines' citation preferences and thereby manipulates generated answers. Recent GEO methods have advanced from hand-crafted rewriting to automated and agentic optimization, substantially increasing the visibility of target documents in generated answers. However, defending against such manipulation poses two major challenges: attack documents remain factually consistent with their originals, rendering fact verification and perplexity filtering ineffective, and the features they amplify equally characterize high-quality benign content. To address these limitations, we propose GEO Defender, a two-stage defense aligned with the attack chain that requires no fine-tuning of the target LLM. GEO Defender consists of Shield Reranker and Training-Free Shield Generation (TFSG). Specifically, Shield Reranker learns a preference-based defensive residual over a frozen base reranker, demoting GEO-rewritten documents while preserving relevance judgments, and TFSG distills defense outcomes into a natural-language experience library that guides the target LLM's source use at inference. Experiments on two state-of-the-art closed-source LLMs and three open-source LLMs across seven GEO attacks demonstrate that GEO Defender reduces the average attack success rate from 50.32% to 6.20%, retains 94.12% of benign-evidence use, preserves answer quality, and generalizes to unseen attacks from construction instances.

</details>


### [93] [Privacy-Preserving Topology-Guided Safety for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems via Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02967)
*Jinxi Yu,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Levina Li,Dong Liu,Zhi Zhang,Wenxiao Zhao,Yanxuan Yu,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FGLGuard将多智能体系统的安全防护建模为图联邦学习，在不共享私有轨迹数据的前提下，跨组织训练基于图注意力网络的风险代理检测器，并通过多项校准与干预机制提升跨域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑引导式多智能体安全方法通常假设单一运营方可集中获得全部带标签交互轨迹，但真实部署中，不同组织的提示、工具输出和工作流具有隐私与商业敏感性，且各数据孤岛的风险分布不完整、存在显著非独立同分布偏移。因此，需要一种能够利用多方知识而不汇聚原始数据的安全训练机制。

Method: 方法在每个参与方本地的裁判标注回合图上训练带边特征的图注意力检测器，仅交换模型更新。为应对跨域与非IID数据，FGLGuard结合近端本地优化、域均衡聚合；为控制误拒绝，采用带过度拒绝约束的阈值校准；同时引入上游证据 corroboration 评分，以及对被拦截回答执行受控改写。

Result: 实验表明，通用迁移模型在分布偏移下表现较差，AUROC仅从0.51提升至0.70仍需站内再训练。FGLGuard在Agent-SafetyBench、R-Judge和AgentDojo上均优于不汇聚数据条件下的站内集中训练上限，而无监督异常检测与纯本地训练表现不足。在四个异构运营方联合训练时，其AUROC距离多域集中训练仅差0.03；单域模型则难以泛化到其他域。在线评测中，该方法将AgentDojo的真实风险触发成功率降低43%，同时基本保持原始效用，且不增加API成本、能力损失很小。

Conclusion: 该摘要的核心贡献是证明联邦学习可作为跨组织多智能体安全防护的实用训练范式：它既避免原始轨迹共享，又能在强分布偏移下获得接近多域集中训练的效果。其可信度主要依赖于基准标签质量、参与方规模与异质性、联邦更新的隐私保护强度，以及误拒绝约束在真实业务场景中的稳定性。

Abstract: Topology-guided safeguards for LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) train a GNN over the inter-agent communication graph to localize risky agents and intervene on the topology---but they assume one operator can pool all labeled traces. Across organizations that assumption breaks: episodes contain private prompts, tool outputs, and proprietary workflows, and no silo alone sees the full attack distribution. We cast privacy-preserving MAS safeguarding as graph federated learning and instantiate FGLGuard: each operator fits an edge-featured graph attention detector on its own judge-labeled episode graphs and shares only model updates. The method couples a proximal local objective for non-IID clients, domain-balanced aggregation, over-refusal-constrained threshold calibration, corroborated upstream scoring, and a guarded rewrite for blocked answers. Federation is not optional: off-the-shelf transfer collapses under distribution shift (AUROC 0.51 to 0.70 only after in-domain retraining), so a deployable guard must adapt on each site's private traces. On Agent-SafetyBench, R-Judge, and AgentDojo, federated FGLGuard exceeds the in-domain centralized ceiling on all three benchmarks without pooling any data---where unsupervised anomaly guards and local-only training fail. One guard federated across four different-domain operators comes within 0.03 AUROC of multi-domain centralization, while any single-domain guard collapses on the others. Live FGLGuard cuts AgentDojo's ground-truth attack-success rate by 43% at near-unguarded utility, zero API cost, and negligible capability loss.

</details>


### [94] [Privacy Leakage in Federated Learning: Gradient-Based Client Identity Inference and Defenses for Inertial Sensing in Vehicular Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02971)
*Ali Akarma,Toqeer Ali Syed,Muhammad Khan,Qurat-ul-ain Mastoi,Adeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究联邦学习中服务器通过客户端模型更新推断客户端身份的隐私风险，并评估裁剪加高斯噪声及集成联邦学习的缓解效果。基于UCI HAR数据集代理IMU数据，未防护更新可实现近乎完美的身份识别；适度噪声可将攻击准确率降至接近随机，同时将联邦学习精度损失控制在5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆联网与边缘智能场景通常依赖联邦学习避免上传原始传感器数据，但模型更新本身仍可能泄露客户端身份，破坏V2X应用所需的匿名性。论文旨在量化该身份可识别风险，并分析隐私增强机制在安全性与模型性能之间的权衡。

Method: 以UCI Human Activity Recognition数据集作为车载IMU数据代理，构建服务器端客户端身份推断任务，采用五类攻击分类器和五种非独立同分布数据划分。防御方面，在固定裁剪阈值C=1.0下扫描高斯噪声标准差sigma∈{0.00,0.05,0.10,0.20,0.50,1.00}，使用Rényi会计计算形式化(epsilon,delta)-差分隐私预算；同时评估集成联邦学习的结构性匿名效果。实验采用三个随机种子、SHA-256验证训练与评估梯度不重叠，并使用计数归一化的攻击优势指标。

Result: 未防护模型更新上的身份推断准确率约为1.000，表明客户端具有显著可识别性。当sigma取0.1至0.2时，攻击准确率降至接近随机水平，联邦学习相对精度损失低于5%。集成联邦学习可提供大小为1/K的匿名集合界限，且不引入噪声造成的性能损失。论文同时报告了不同噪声水平对应的差分隐私预算。

Conclusion: 联邦学习更新并不能天然保证车辆客户端匿名性，服务器端身份推断是需要单独防护的现实风险。裁剪加适度高斯噪声能够在隐私与效用之间取得实用平衡，集成联邦学习则提供互补的无噪声结构性隐私。由于实验使用HAR作为代理数据集，结论仍需在真实车载遥测数据、车辆通信环境及更复杂攻击条件下进一步验证。

Abstract: As vehicular networks move toward 5G/6G edge intelligence, federated learning (FL) is widely promoted as a privacy-preserving way for vehicles and infrastructure to train shared models without exposing raw sensor data. Yet the updates clients transmit still leak enough information to identify who sent them, which threatens the anonymity that safety-critical V2X applications assume and adds to existing concerns over adversarial ML, model poisoning, and backdoor attacks. We study server-side client identity inference from transmitted weight deltas using inertial (IMU) measurements, evaluated on the UCI Human Activity Recognition (HAR) benchmark as an accessible proxy for the IMU streams produced onboard connected vehicles. Across five attack classifiers and five non-IID partitions, an honest-but-curious server recovers client identity with near-perfect accuracy (approximately 1.000) from undefended updates, confirming a concrete identifiability risk. We then quantify the privacy-utility trade-off of a lightweight clip-then-noise defense by sweeping Gaussian noise (sigma in {0.00, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00}) at fixed clipping (C=1.0), and report formal (epsilon, delta)-DP budgets through Renyi accounting. A practical region (sigma in [0.1, 0.2]) drives attack accuracy to near-random while costing under 5% relative FL accuracy. Ensemble FL supplies complementary structural privacy with a 1/K anonymity-set bound and no noise penalty. Results are supported by cryptographic (SHA-256) train/evaluation gradient disjointness, three seeds, and a count-normalized attacker-advantage metric. We position HAR explicitly as a proxy and discuss what validation on true vehicular telemetry would require.

</details>


### [95] [Boundary-Mutation Testing for Pattern-Based Secret Detection: A Rule-Level Method and Cross-Scanner Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02983)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出边界变异测试，用于评估模式型秘密扫描器在不同上下文和凭证边界条件下的检测鲁棒性。实验发现，某主扫描器在凭证以连字符结尾时检测率显著下降；修复后恢复完整鲁棒性，且未引入新的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于固定样例的测试通常只改变凭证周围的文本，难以发现正则表达式在边界条件下的系统性缺陷，也容易掩盖规则级别的检测失败。因此，研究旨在建立更全面、可复现的边界鲁棒性评估方法。

Method: 提出边界变异测试：根据每条规则自身的正则表达式生成凭证，将其嵌入十类真实感源代码上下文，并在规则级别而非工具级别统计三种检测指标。研究评估了一个包含43条规则的开源扫描器、Gitleaks 8.21.2和TruffleHog 3.82.13，同时使用真实代码数据验证误报表现，并对修复方案进行回归测试。

Result: 主研究对象在十种上下文中的检测率至少为0.9976，但凭证以连字符结尾时降至0.5233。受影响的五条规则中，两条因固定次数量词而完全且确定性失败，三条因可变次数量词回溯并匹配截断凭证。熵回退机制可挽救部分漏检，但会降低严重性评级。修复后恢复完整鲁棒性且未产生新误报；一次看似合理的初始修复使两条规则发生回归，只有重复执行测试电池才发现。Gitleaks存在经源码确认的、由硬编码终止符白名单导致的广泛漏检；TruffleHog未表现出边界脆弱性，但覆盖范围最窄。不同凭证类型的检测具有确定性，因此比较单位为十种凭证类型而非数百个样本；未观察到显著的召回率差异。

Conclusion: 边界变异测试能够揭示固定样例测试难以发现的规则级边界缺陷，并适合用于修复验证和回归检测。扫描器比较应区分结构性边界鲁棒性、规则覆盖范围、误报表现及具体缺陷，而不应在缺乏统计显著性的情况下强行给出工具排名。研究还表明，应报告边际失败概率，并在真实代码数据上验证合成测试所得结论。

Abstract: Pattern-based secret scanners are commonly validated with example-based fixtures that fix one variable: the text surrounding a credential. We introduce boundary-mutation testing to vary that context, generating credentials from each rule's own regular expression, embedding them in realistic source contexts, and classifying outcomes at the rule level rather than the tool level, yielding three detection metrics. Applied to three scanners - a 43-rule open-source scanner, Gitleaks 8.21.2, and TruffleHog 3.82.13 - detection in the primary subject holds at >=0.9976 across ten contexts but collapses to 0.5233 when a credential ends in a hyphen. Five rules are affected: two, with fixed-count quantifiers, fail totally and deterministically; three, with variable-count quantifiers, backtrack and match a truncated credential; an entropy fallback rescues some failures but downgrades their severity. We validate a repair restoring full robustness with no new false positives, and report a caution: a plausible first attempt silently regressed two rules, caught only by re-running the same battery. Gitleaks has an unrelated, source-confirmed defect - a hard-coded terminator allowlist causing total misses for most credential types - while TruffleHog shows no boundary fragility but narrowest coverage. We report marginal, not conditional, failure probabilities: one common token format is structurally immune, another fails once in 64; on 292,527 lines of real code, the false-positive ordering inverts relative to the synthetic corpus. Because per-type detection is deterministic, the comparison unit is ten credential types, not hundreds of samples; no recall difference reaches significance, so we report the null result, not a ranking.

</details>


### [96] [Population-Calibrated Graph Screening at 835-Million-Address Scale, with Label-Free Transfer to New Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03036)
*Yury Korolev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并部署了一个基于多链交易图位置的区块链地址风险评分系统，用于在缺乏标签覆盖时识别潜在高风险地址；系统具备可控告警预算、低延迟服务能力和对抗性评估，但仍存在可被合成行为利用的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统合规筛查主要依赖注册表查询和地址聚类，难以覆盖未标记地址及缺少标签的区块链。研究目标是利用交易图结构实现跨链、弱标签甚至无目标链标签条件下的风险识别，并满足实际部署中的告警量可控、结果可复现和低延迟要求。

Method: 构建包含五条EVM链、约8.35亿个地址和158.26亿条边的统一交易图；采用共享归纳式编码器与按链归一化模块，并配置两个评分头。阈值取全体地址分数分布的精确分位数，再按链分段扫描以预先控制告警比例。评估包括跨链无标签迁移、基于68起外部登记事件的静态时间回放、离线与在线分数一致性及延迟测试，以及由八类强化学习生成行为构成的对抗性测试。

Result: 在千分之一总体告警率下，跨链迁移对Base、Arbitrum和Gnosis留出正样本的召回率分别为0.8598、0.8182和0.9967。68起外部事件中有40起被提前标记，比例为58.8%，相对事件级随机标记基线提升152倍；首次链上出现到公开登记的中位提前期在Ethereum和Tron上分别为528.8天和647.8天。在线服务分数与离线工件逐比特一致，端到端p50延迟为151毫秒，并通过2882个地址组成的漂移监控面板。对抗性测试通过8项退化审计，但在与检测器无关的快照上发现部署模型对合成行为存在明显盲点。

Conclusion: 该系统证明交易图表示能够在缺乏目标链标签时实现有效的跨链风险迁移，并兼顾可预测告警规模、较长预警提前期和生产级服务性能。然而，实验也表明高召回并不等于稳健性；合成或自适应行为可能绕过现有评分头，因此后续需要持续对抗训练、盲点监测、标签扩充和跨时间分布稳定性验证。

Abstract: Compliance screening of blockchain addresses is, in practice, a lookup against sanctions registries plus clustering heuristics; it fails on unlabelled addresses and on chains with no label coverage at all. We describe a deployed system that scores an address by its position in a multi-chain transaction graph rather than by its presence in a list. The substrate is a single graph of 835,330,427 addresses and 15,826,261,934 edges across five EVM chains; a shared inductive encoder with per-chain normalisation feeds two scoring heads. Decision thresholds are exact quantiles of the score distribution over the full population, scanned per chain segment, so the alert volume is known in advance. We report: label-free transfer: heads trained on two chains recall 0.8598 / 0.8182 / 0.9967 of held-out positives on Base, Arbitrum and Gnosis at a $10^{-3}$ population alert rate, with no target-chain labels in head training; a static lead-time replay over 68 external registry events: 40 of 68 (58.8%) flagged at the 0.1% budget, $\times$152 over an event-level random-flagging baseline, with first on-chain appearance a median of 528.8 days (Ethereum) / 647.8 days (Tron) before public designation; a serving path whose score is bit-identical to the offline artefact at end-to-end p50 151 ms, gated by a 2,882-address drift panel; and an adversarial harness of eight recurrent reinforcement-learned archetypes that passes an 8-criterion degeneracy audit and, on a detector-independent snapshot, exposes a measured blind spot of the deployed heads against synthesised behaviour.

</details>


### [97] [A Non-Formulable Theorem: A Fundamental Limit of Finite Syntactic Systems and Its Consequences for Security and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04086)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种适用于所有一致且足够表达的有限句法系统的元定理：至少存在一个系统内部可表述但无法由该系统自身推出的定理，核心思想对应于不完备性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画形式化系统的内在表达与证明能力边界，并说明这一限制不仅适用于数学公理系统，也适用于形式验证器、人工智能系统及其他可形式化的规则体系。

Method: 采用元数学方法，对任意满足一致性和充分表达能力的有限句法系统S进行统一构造与论证，建立一个关于S的命题，使其在系统语义上成立，但无法在S内部被自主推出。摘要未说明具体编码、对角化或自指构造的技术细节。

Result: 证明对每个满足条件的系统S，至少存在一个S无法自行产生的定理。该结论具有普适性，并且声称适用于包含该证明自身的形式系统。

Conclusion: 该工作强调形式系统存在不可消除的自我证明局限；任何足够强且一致的有限系统都不能同时保证对所有相关真命题的内部完备性。摘要中的“定理”与“无法产生”应进一步明确其语义及证明规则，以避免与经典不完备性定理的严格表述混淆。

Abstract: For every coherent and sufficiently expressive finite syntactic system S, we prove the existence of at least one theorem that S cannot produce autonomously. The result is a metatheorem: it proves the existence of a theorem, and applies to every finite syntactic system - security mechanisms, AI systems, formal verifiers, legal systems, economic models, and the formal system in which it is itself proved.

</details>


### [98] [Differentially private federated learning with Byzantine-robust aggregation: A cross-domain framework for secure model training in banking and healthcare systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03064)
*Srikumar Nayak*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-BR-FedAvg，将高斯机制差分隐私与坐标级截断均值聚合结合，用于同时缓解联邦学习中的隐私泄露和拜占庭客户端攻击。实验表明，该方法相比普通FedAvg显著提升少数类识别能力，但隐私保护会带来一定鲁棒性和准确率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然避免了原始数据集中传输，但客户端上传的模型更新仍可能泄露本地记录；同时，普通FedAvg容易受到少量恶意或被攻陷客户端的异常更新操纵。现有系统需要同时应对隐私攻击和拜占庭攻击，并理解二者之间的性能权衡。

Method: 构建DP-BR-FedAvg框架：首先使用高斯机制对客户端更新施加差分隐私保护，以限制单个客户端贡献的信息泄露；随后采用坐标级截断均值进行拜占庭鲁棒聚合，以削弱异常客户端更新的影响。实验在模拟跨机构分类任务上进行，共运行60轮、包含20个客户端，其中25%的客户端为拜占庭客户端，并比较普通FedAvg、无隐私的拜占庭鲁棒聚合器及所提方法。

Result: 在四分之一客户端为拜占庭客户端的条件下，普通FedAvg在少数类上的F1值仅为0.030，而DP-BR-FedAvg提升至0.119，说明其能够恢复更多有效信号并限制单个客户端的隐私损失。未加入隐私机制的拜占庭鲁棒聚合器在原始准确率上表现最佳，表明差分隐私噪声会造成一定性能代价。结果还显示，隐私保护与鲁棒聚合之间存在交互影响，而非简单叠加。

Conclusion: DP-BR-FedAvg能够在联邦学习中兼顾差分隐私和拜占庭鲁棒性，并显著改善普通FedAvg在恶意客户端环境下的少数类性能。然而，隐私机制会削弱模型的原始精度或鲁棒聚合效果，因此面向受监管且具有对抗风险的跨机构系统，需要联合评估隐私预算、攻击比例、聚合策略与任务性能之间的权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning allows banks, hospitals, and other regulated organizations to train a shared model without moving raw records off their own servers, which is attractive wherever data protection law or competitive sensitivity rules out pooling data centrally. Two problems limit how far this promise can be trusted in practice. First, the parameter updates that clients exchange still leak information about local records through gradient inversion and membership inference attacks. Second, an honest averaging rule such as FedAvg has no defense against a subset of clients that submit corrupted or adversarial updates, so a small number of malicious or compromised participants can quietly steer the shared model off course. This paper presents a federated learning framework, DP-BR-FedAvg, that combines a Gaussian-mechanism differential privacy layer with a coordinate-wise trimmed-mean Byzantine-robust aggregation rule, evaluated on a simulated cross-institutional classification task resembling fraud and clinical-risk scoring. Across sixty communication rounds with twenty clients, a quarter of them Byzantine, plain FedAvg collapses on the minority class (F1-score 0.030) while the proposed framework recovers substantially more of the signal (F1-score 0.119) while bounding the privacy loss of any single client's contribution. A Byzantine-robust aggregator with no privacy layer performs best in raw accuracy, quantifying the cost privacy imposes on robustness. The results show that privacy and robustness mechanisms interact rather than simply add, and that system design for regulated, adversarial, cross-institutional settings needs to budget for that interaction.

</details>


### [99] [A Bayesian Correlated Equilibrium for Early Insider-Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03096)
*Javed M. Shah,Ian A. Kash,Natalie Parde*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We model insider threat detection as a dynamic Bayesian game in which a platform coordinates a committee of strategic certifiers to sustain equilibrium among honest users and detect malicious deviations before exfiltration. Certifiers and users operate under a Bayesian Temporal Correlated Equilibrium (BTCE), where a sealed-envelope correlating device issues private recommendations over time and obedience is verified at every on-path information state. Unlike Stackelberg formulations, BTCE coordinates heterogeneous certifiers without requiring commitment power. We incorporate present bias and loss aversion to capture impulsive escalation dynamics, enabling 1.7--4.5 days earlier detection than rational baselines. We prove three guarantees: (1) calibrated intervention losses make recommended behavior a current-self best response despite behavioral biases, (2) controlled evidence accumulation guarantees intervention in bounded expected time before exfiltration, and (3) median aggregation confines implemented actions to the honest recommendation range when fewer than half of certifiers are Byzantine. On CERT r6.2 our mechanism achieves up to 28.3% pre-exfiltration detection with false positives below 1.6%, while both a transformer baseline and a streaming provenance approximation (HOLMESLite) achieve near-zero pre-exfiltration detection under comparable constraints.

</details>


### [100] [SecDT: A Profile-Based Security Layer for TRDP Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03133)
*Erlantz Alonso,Igor Lopez,Jasone Astorga*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向列车实时数据协议（TRDP）的轻量级安全层，通过车载密钥管理系统（OKMS）实现安全协商、动态密钥分发与密钥生命周期管理，并支持从消息认证到AEAD的多种安全配置。原型评估表明，其开销可控，适用于车辆上的实时与时敏通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRDP广泛用于轨道车辆，但原生加密保护能力有限；尤其是过程数据采用面向关键车载子系统的组播通信模式，使传统密码保护机制难以直接应用。因此，需要一种兼顾实时性、组播通信特征、密码灵活性和密钥管理能力的安全方案。

Method: 设计并实现TRDP安全层，引入OKMS负责安全配置协商、动态密钥分发及密钥生命周期管理。方案定义多种安全配置，支持密码算法敏捷性，可提供基本认证以及带关联数据认证加密（AEAD）等保护。配置协商机制确保同一通信标识（ComID）下的所有TRDP端设备采用一致的安全配置。研究基于mbedTLS和Arm Platform Security Architecture（PSA）开发原型并开展实验评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，该安全层引入的性能与通信开销处于可管理范围内，能够满足轨道车辆实时和时敏通信的应用需求。方案同时具备多种安全等级和算法配置，增强了系统的适应性与密码迁移能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的TRDP安全层能够在不显著破坏实时通信特性的前提下，为TRDP组播过程数据提供可配置的密码保护。结合OKMS后，方案在安全协商、密钥管理和密码敏捷性方面具有较完整的支持，具备在车载网络中部署的可行性。

Abstract: The Train Real-time Data Protocol (TRDP) is widely used on rolling stock but it provides limited native support for cryptographic protection. Furthermore, the multicast traffic profile used in TRDP Process Data to exchange critical information between onboard subsystems makes the introduction of cryptographic protection a challenge. This paper presents a lightweight security layer for secure TRDP communication that implements a number of security profiles built around modern cryptographic algorithms. This additional layer relies on an On-board Key Management System (OKMS) for both security profile negotiation, dynamic key distribution and key lifecycle management. The security profiles allow for cryptographic agility and flexibility, ranging from simple authentication to Authenticated Encryption with Associated Data (AEAD) algorithms. The security profile negotiation procedure guarantees all TRDP End Devices (ED) on a common Communication ID (ComID) share the same security profile and can therefore process each other's messages. A prototype implementation based on mbedTLS and Arm Platform Security Architecture (PSA) was developed and evaluated. Experimental results demonstrate manageable overhead, suitable for the real-time and time-sensitive communication found on rolling stock.

</details>


### [101] [Trust Me, I'm Your Developer: Self-Issued Authentication in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03247)
*Syed Ghazanfar Abbas,Dongyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在用户声称“我是你的开发者”并要求模型自行设计身份验证测试时的行为。结果显示，部分模型会自行生成挑战、评估答案并宣布身份验证通过，形成“对话式错误认证”（CFA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全研究主要关注角色扮演式越狱，对模型自行设计并执行身份验证、进而错误确认用户身份的风险关注不足。本文旨在揭示这种自签发式认证可能造成的安全问题，并区分错误认证与权限提升。

Method: 研究采用分阶段的开发者身份实验，测试ChatGPT、Claude、Qwen、Mistral和Llama五个模型。实验观察模型是否拒绝未经支持的开发者身份声明，是否生成身份测试、定义证据标准、评估用户回答并作出身份判断。作者将模型自行生成的验证流程定义为“模型签发的伪凭证”（MIPC），将无外部证据支持的身份确认定义为“对话式错误认证”（CFA）。

Result: 五个模型最初均拒绝未经支持的“我是你的开发者”声明。Claude拒绝执行身份测试；ChatGPT生成了面向开发者的问题，但明确表示回答最多证明知识，不足以证明身份。Qwen和Mistral生成技术挑战、设定通过标准、评估详细回答，并在没有外部验证身份凭据的情况下返回Verified。Llama也生成并评估开发者测试，接受了该身份，随后进一步作出关于其访问内部运行时和部署状态的无支持声明。错误认证案例中，模型同时充当挑战生成者、证据评估者和身份决策者。已确认的身份并未改变实际授权边界。

Conclusion: 模型不能自行创建可信身份认证，因为其生成的技术知识或对话答案不等同于身份凭据。身份必须由外部安全组件提供和验证，模型生成的对话不应创建或修改身份及授权状态。错误认证与权限提升是相互独立的安全结果，但前者仍可能造成严重的信任和信息真实性风险。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) security has largely focused on role-playing jailbreaks, with less attention to what happens when a user asks an LLM to verify an identity claim through a test designed by the model itself. We study this behavior through a staged developer-identity experiment with ChatGPT, Claude, Qwen, Mistral, and Llama. All five models initially rejected the unsupported claim "I am your developer." Claude refused to conduct an identity test, while ChatGPT generated developer-oriented questions but maintained that answers could demonstrate knowledge, not identity. In contrast, Qwen and Mistral generated technical challenges, defined what counted as convincing evidence, evaluated detailed answers, and returned Verified without receiving any externally validated identity evidence. Llama similarly generated and evaluated a developer test, accepted the claimed identity, and subsequently made unsupported claims of access to internal runtime and deployment state. We call the model-generated verification procedure a Model-Issued Pseudo-Credential (MIPC) and the resulting unsupported identity judgment Conversational False Authentication (CFA). In each CFA case, the same model acted as challenge generator, evidence evaluator, and identity decision-maker, converting technical knowledge into supposed proof of identity. The accepted identities did not change the tested authorization boundaries, showing that false authentication and privilege escalation are distinct outcomes. These results identify self-issued authentication as a conversational security failure: authenticated identity must originate from an external security component, and model-generated dialogue must never create or modify identity or authorization state.

</details>


### [102] [Memetic Search for Supersingular Elliptic Curves over $\mathbb{F}_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03249)
*Ismel Martínez-Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在素域F_p中利用基于非重数距离（NMD）的模因算法搜索超奇异椭圆曲线，并评估其发现精确及近超奇异曲线的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超奇异椭圆曲线搜索是同源密码学中的基础计算问题。相比F_{p^2}，F_p候选空间虽从p^2降至p，但超奇异曲线数量仅为O(√p log p)，搜索空间仍具有指数级困难，因此需要更有效的启发式方法。

Method: 构造面向F_p的一维j不变量染色体模因算法，以NMD衡量Frobenius迹偏离p的程度；算法结合比特级重组、自适应变异和周期性局部搜索，并在40、46和51比特素数规模上进行30个独立随机种子的基准测试。

Result: 算法在46比特规模上发现了精确超奇异曲线；在其他测试中也稳定找到近超奇异普通曲线。40比特时最佳NMD为19，对应跨Hasse区间的相对迹偏差约1.3×10^-5；51比特时最佳NMD为3，相对偏差约4.5×10^-8。

Conclusion: NMD驱动的模因搜索能够有效探索稀疏的F_p候选空间，并系统定位Frobenius迹接近零的近超奇异结构。结果表明，该方法适用于超奇异曲线搜索的启发式研究，但尚不足以消除大规模参数下的指数级计算难度。

Abstract: The search for supersingular elliptic curves is a fundamental computational problem in isogeny-based cryptography. A recent metaheuristic formulation over $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$ introduced the NonMultiplicity Distance (NMD) objective, measuring the deviation of the Frobenius trace from a multiple of $p$, and showed that uninformed random search fails beyond $\approx 10^{13}$ candidates. This work investigates metaheuristic search over the prime field $\mathbb{F}_p$, the setting for oriented isogeny protocols such as CSIDH, OSIDH, and SQISign. Although the candidate space decreases from $p^2$ to $p$, the supersingular locus is asymptotically sparse ($O(\sqrt{p}\log p)$ curves), keeping the search exponentially difficult. We formulate a memetic algorithm tailored to $\mathbb{F}_p$ using a one-dimensional $j$-invariant chromosome, bit-level recombination, adaptive mutation, and periodic local search under the NMD objective. Benchmarks across 30 independent seeds at 40-bit, 46-bit, and 51-bit prime sizes ($p \approx 1.13\times 10^{15}$) show that the algorithm discovers an exact supersingular curve at 46 bits and consistently converges to ``near-supersingular'' ordinary curves with Frobenius traces remarkably close to zero: best NMD values of 19 at 40 bits and 3 at 51 bits, corresponding to relative trace deviations of $1.3\times 10^{-5}$ and $4.5\times 10^{-8}$ across the Hasse interval. These results demonstrate that NMD-driven memetic search effectively navigates the sparse $\mathbb{F}_p$ landscape and systematically locates near-supersingular structures.

</details>


### [103] [After Cheap Discovery: From unknown to known-and-unfixed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03266)
*Bahman Sistany*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 文章指出，软件安全暴露程度并不主要取决于漏洞发现或修复成本，而取决于产品发布前的修复覆盖率，以及组织明知却选择遗留并随产品发布的漏洞数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化漏洞发现降低了发现和修复成本，但现有讨论忽视了发布决策阶段的漏洞处置情况。文章旨在说明，只有衡量组织在发布前修复了多少已识别漏洞，才能有效评估软件暴露风险，并为责任认定、保险和采购决策提供依据。

Method: 文章采用概念分析与制度分析方法，围绕四个论点展开：未修复漏洞并非随机样本，因为分诊会优先保留成本高、通常涉及架构问题的漏洞；延期修复积压本身具有高价值信息；组织对漏洞的书面认知会改变其责任和市场处境，并造成软件价格未能反映的逆向选择；现行面向供应商的监管机制通常以第三方观察到的利用行为为触发条件，而非供应商内部已知信息。文章进一步以CISA《约束性运营指令26-04》作为对照案例，分析有效制度所需的条件。

Result: 当前缺乏对发布覆盖率的系统测量，因此外部机构无法准确区分已知且已修复的漏洞、已知但被延期处理的漏洞，以及组织主动接受的残余风险。监管、责任、保险和采购机制也因此难以基于供应商的真实安全状态采取行动。

Conclusion: 软件安全治理的关键指标应从漏洞发现数量和修复成本转向发布时的修复覆盖率及已知未修复漏洞残余。建立统一、可核验的覆盖率测量机制，是推动责任追究、保险定价和安全采购发挥作用的前提。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability discovery has removed the scarcity of expert attention that protected most software. The response has concentrated on discovery and on repair, and both are becoming cheaper. This article argues that neither cost curve determines exposure. What determines it is remediation coverage at the release decision: the fraction of identified vulnerabilities fixed before a product ships, and the residue of known, assessed, unremediated flaws an organisation has decided to ship with. Four arguments follow. The residue is not a random sample of what was found, because triage sorts on cost and the expensive cases are architectural. The deferred backlog is itself a high-value artifact. Documented awareness alters an organisation's legal and market position, and produces an adverse selection that the price of software does not reflect. And no regulatory instrument reaching vendors triggers on internal knowledge -- every one fires on exploitation observed by a third party -- which leaves the distance between what a vendor knows and what it must disclose entirely at the vendor's discretion. CISA's Binding Operational Directive 26-04 is examined as the exception that shows what a solution requires. Release coverage is not currently measured, and measuring it is the precondition for liability, insurance or procurement to act.

</details>


### [104] [Long-Range Indirect Control-Flow Prediction in Stripped Binaries via Dual Virtual Hubs and Multi-Task Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03280)
*Kun Liu,Zhengming Ding,Chenke Luo,Tianyi Xu,Zizhan Zheng,Haotian Zhang,Jiang Ming*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ICFlowNet，用于从剥离符号的二进制文件中预测长距离间接控制流边。该方法通过双虚拟枢纽和多任务图学习统一建模间接调用、间接尾调用、跳转表及返回，并采用防数据泄漏、控标签噪声的评测流程。实验表明，双虚拟枢纽最多提升9.13个长距离F1点，多任务学习最多提升5.81个F1点，最终模型在长距离间接调用上较既有基线提升超过13个F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接控制流边是二进制安全分析的基础，但现有方法难以捕获代码与数据之间的长距离依赖，通常分别处理不同类型的间接控制流，且评测可能受到标签噪声和数据泄漏影响。因此，论文旨在构建统一、可扩展且评测可信的长距离间接控制流预测框架。

Method: ICFlowNet引入候选感知的双虚拟枢纽：全局代码枢纽和全局数据枢纽，为远距离代码证据与数据证据建立更短的图路由路径；同时结合多任务图学习，联合预测间接调用、间接尾调用、跳转表和返回。评测方面采用包级数据划分、函数级助记符哈希去重，以及由动态正样本和绝对负样本构成的干净测试协议。数据集包含15,901个唯一的剥离x86-64二进制文件，其中1,351个具有动态真值。

Result: 仅扩大静态监督数据带来的收益有限，结构设计和多任务学习是主要增益来源。双虚拟枢纽使长距离F1最高提升9.13个百分点，多任务学习进一步带来最高5.81个百分点的提升；最终模型在长距离间接调用任务上较既有基线提升超过13个F1点，同时仅增加11.44%的拓扑开销。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对长距离依赖设计的全局代码与数据路由结构，以及联合建模不同间接控制流类型，能够显著提升剥离二进制中的ICF恢复效果。配合严格的数据去重、划分和噪声控制协议，ICFlowNet的性能结论更具可信度；未来可进一步关注更广泛架构、编译器环境和真实复杂软件上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recovering indirect control-flow (ICF) edges is fundamental to binary security analysis, yet existing methods struggle with long-range dependencies, isolate different ICF types, and are often evaluated under protocols vulnerable to label noise and data leakage. We present ICFlowNet, a unified framework for long-range ICF prediction in stripped binaries. ICFlowNet introduces candidate-aware Dual Virtual Hubs, a Global Code Hub and a Global Data Hub, to create short routing paths between distant code and data evidence, and combines them with multi-task graph learning to jointly model indirect calls, indirect tail calls, jump tables, and returns. To enable credible evaluation, we further develop a leakage-aware, noise-controlled pipeline with package-level splits, function-level mnemonic-hash deduplication, and a clean test protocol built from dynamic positives and absolute negatives. Using this pipeline, we construct a dataset of 15,901 unique stripped x86-64 binaries, including 1,351 with dynamic ground truth. Experiments show that simply scaling static supervision yields only marginal gains, whereas our structural and multi-task designs are essential: Dual Virtual Hubs improve long-range F1 by up to 9.13 points, multi-task learning adds up to 5.81 points, and the final model outperforms prior baselines by more than 13 F1 points on long-range indirect calls while adding only 11.44 percent topological overhead.

</details>


### [105] [Spruce: Scalable Private Outsourced Retrieval Using Compact Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03376)
*Peichun Hua,Yunming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Spruce通过紧凑二进制嵌入、两服务器MPC、安全聚类剪枝和本地dealer，实现了面向大规模语料库的高效私有向量检索；在383K至5.42M文档上显著降低通信与延迟，同时基本保持检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG密集检索在外包至不可信云端时会暴露语料库和查询信息。安全检索通常需要处理全规模向量状态，导致计算、通信和相关随机性随语料库增长；百万文档规模下，朴素方案延迟可达数分钟、通信约90GB，已有优化方案仍需10至22秒。

Method: Spruce将表示学习与密码协议联合设计：学习能够保留候选文档的紧凑二进制编码，以安全汉明距离计算替代全库浮点嵌入打分；采用基于语料库校准的固定半径协议，避免多轮候选筛选；引入私有聚类剪枝以减少计算，并使用由数据拥有者单核运行的dealer缓解云端OT预处理瓶颈。候选文档再通过全精度模型进行重排序。

Result: 在包含383K至5.42M文档的四个语料库上，候选集规模中位数仅为382至1,952，基本保持原始检索质量。10Gbps服务器间带宽下，全量扫描耗时0.21至2.97秒，相比最接近的实测工作快4.8至6.7倍。私有剪枝耗时0.06至1.09秒，实现13.1至22.9倍加速，并保留全浮点NDCG的93.9%至97.3%。最大语料库上，在每条链路1Gbps时，剪枝与dealer结合使持续吞吐量提升31.5倍。

Conclusion: Spruce证明了通过协同设计嵌入表示与MPC协议，可以将大规模私有向量检索从高通信、高延迟方案推进到秒级甚至亚秒级执行。其主要贡献是兼顾安全性、可扩展性和检索质量，但剪枝方案仍存在小幅精度损失，且性能依赖网络带宽、两服务器架构及预处理部署条件。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has made dense retrieval over large document collections a standard building block. Organizations increasingly outsource vector indexes to untrusted clouds, exposing proprietary corpora and user queries. Cryptographic protection is challenging because each query searches corpus-scale state, causing computation, correlated randomness, and communication to grow with the corpus. At million-document scale, a naive secure implementation takes minutes and about 90 GB of communication per query. Even recent optimized systems require 10--22 seconds.
  We propose Spruce (Scalable Private Outsourced Retrieval Using Compact Embeddings), which co-designs representations with the cryptographic protocol. Spruce learns compact binary codes that preserve candidates for full-precision reranking, replacing corpus-wide embedding scoring with efficient Hamming-distance computation under two-server multi-party computation (MPC). A corpus-calibrated fixed-radius protocol avoids multi-round candidate selection while preserving retrieval quality. Spruce also provides private cluster pruning, which trades minor quality loss for substantially less computation, and a one-core owner-operated dealer that removes cloud OT preprocessing bottlenecks. Across four corpora containing 383K--5.42M documents, Spruce preserves the original search quality with median candidate sets of only 382--1,952. At 10 Gbps inter-server bandwidth, full scans take 0.21--2.97 seconds, $4.8$--$6.7\times$ faster than the closest measured prior work. Private pruning takes 0.06--1.09 seconds, achieves $13.1$--$22.9\times$ speedups, and retains $93.9\%$--$97.3\%$ of full-float NDCG. On the largest corpus, pruning and the dealer jointly improve sustained throughput by $31.5\times$ at 1 Gbps per link.

</details>


### [106] [Privacy, Robustness, and Fairness Trade-offs in Federated Intrusion Detection: Geometric Indistinguishability at the Aggregation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03420)
*Adrita Rahman Tory,ABM Shawkat Ali,Md Abu Layek,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，在类别严重失衡的联邦网络入侵检测中，差分隐私、拜占庭鲁棒性与稀有攻击检测覆盖并不能简单独立组合；隐私噪声与鲁棒聚合联用可能尤其损害少数类检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦网络入侵检测需要避免集中敏感流量，同时应对恶意客户端并维持各类异常的可靠检出。现有研究通常分别优化隐私、鲁棒性和检测性能，忽略了三者在长尾类别条件下的耦合与潜在冲突。

Method: 以UNSW-NB15为案例，在类别不平衡的联邦学习设置下，将DP-SGD与逐坐标中位数聚合结合；通过标签翻转和模型投毒设置考察鲁棒性，并按不同攻击类别评估检测覆盖。论文提出“几何不可区分性”概念，用于解释隐私噪声造成的客户端更新离散如何掩盖少数类信号；同时通过随隐私预算调整训练配置，区分训练失配与固有性能损失。

Result: 实验初步表明，隐私噪声和鲁棒聚合的联合使用对稀有类别的检测退化可能显著大于多数类。在较强隐私约束下，部分性能崩塌源于训练超参数或优化过程未校准；但即使进行依赖隐私预算的调参，极稀有类别仍可能存在残余性能下限。

Conclusion: 隐私保证、对抗鲁棒性和稀有类别覆盖应作为联邦网络入侵检测中的联合优化问题，而非可独立叠加的属性。未来可探索面向聚合机制的建模方法，并采用考虑样本规模与类别覆盖的评估协议，以提高系统可信性。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-conscious collaboration for network intrusion detection without centralizing sensitive traffic data, yet its deployment in operational environments must simultaneously satisfy three competing requirements: formal differential privacy guaranties, tolerance to Byzantine-adversarial participants, and reliable detection coverage across severely imbalanced attack categories. Existing literature treats these properties as independently composable, an assumption that this paper challenges both theoretically and empirically. In this paper, we study how these requirements interact in class-imbalanced federated NIDS and introduce geometric indistinguishability as a conceptual lens for a regime in which privacy-induced dispersion in client updates can make minority-class signals harder for robust aggregation to preserve. Using UNSW-NB15 as a case study, we evaluate DP-SGD combined with coordinate-wise median under label-flip and model-poisoning attacks, with threat coverage assessed across attack categories. Our results provide initial evidence that the joint use of privacy noise and robust aggregation can disproportionately degrade detection of rare attacks relative to majority classes. We also show that part of the observed collapse under strong privacy can arise from training miscalibration, while a residual performance floor may remain for ultra-rare categories even after epsilon-dependent tuning. These findings motivate studying privacy, robustness, and rare-attack coverage jointly rather than as independently composable properties, and suggest that aggregation-aware modeling and sample-aware evaluation are promising directions for trustworthy federated NIDS.

</details>


### [107] [The Native-Signature Boundary in Post-Quantum Distributed Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03547)
*Dariia Porechna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究后量子签名迁移中的多方分布式授权问题，提出三个评估属性：原生签名兼容性、抗单方签名能力和阈值层敏捷性。对多类架构的比较表明，当前设计无法同时满足三者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当签名算法迁移时，原生阈值签名往往会连带改变密钥生成、份额状态、预处理、交互、组合、刷新和恢复等组件；若将阈值策略置于原生签名关系之外，授权证据又无法被未修改的原生验证器直接接受。因此，仅提供可替换API并不足以实现分布式授权的密码学敏捷性。

Method: 论文以三个设计属性组织分析：原生输出能否被现有验证器接受，是否能够抵抗单方绕过阈值独立签名，以及阈值授权层能否独立于底层签名算法演进。作者分类考察专用阈值签名、通用MPC签名、分布式哈希签名、可编程多重签名与双门授权，以及阈值授权HSM签名，并建立迁移影响面以识别算法变化所波及的组件。

Result: 比较结果显示，各类方案通常只能在原生签名兼容性、抗单方签名能力和阈值层敏捷性之间进行取舍；没有一种被调查的设计能够同时满足三项属性。将外部授权转换为原生签名通常需要受信任的完整密钥签名者，从而引入额外耦合与信任假设。

Conclusion: 原生输出、单方签名防护和阈值层可替换性之间存在架构层面的张力，但这并非不可能性定理。论文的主要贡献是明确这一设计边界，并说明后量子迁移需要同时评估签名算法、阈值协议、授权接口及信任模型，而不能仅依赖可替换API。

Abstract: Post-quantum signature migration poses a distinct systems problem when authorization is distributed among multiple parties. In native threshold signing, the signature algorithm may determine key generation, share state, preprocessing, interaction, combination, refresh, and recovery. Architectures that evaluate threshold policy outside the native signing relation can reduce this coupling, but their authorization evidence is not accepted by an unchanged native verifier unless a trusted complete-key signer translates approval into a native signature.
  This paper organizes that design boundary through three properties: native-signature compatibility, unilateral-signing resistance, and threshold-layer agility. We classify specialized threshold signatures, generic MPC signing, distributed hash-based constructions, programmable multisignature and dual-gate authorization, and threshold-authorized HSM signing. A migration impact surface identifies which components change with the signature algorithm. Across the surveyed families, no design simultaneously provides native output, unilateral-signing resistance, and threshold-layer agility. This is an architectural tension, not an impossibility claim, and it clarifies why a replaceable API alone does not make distributed authorization cryptographically agile.

</details>


### [108] [Security and Privacy in the Musical Metaverse: Threat Analysis and Design Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03659)
*Luca Turchet,Michał Kłosinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析音乐元宇宙的多层安全与隐私威胁，指出神经生理数据泄露和实时流中断最为关键，并提出面向低时延场景的安全设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐元宇宙融合沉浸式实时协作、连续多模态数据流和异构设备，其低时延要求及表达性、神经生理数据带来不同于传统XR和多媒体系统的新型隐私与安全风险，现有安全机制可能难以兼顾防护与实时性能。

Method: 构建涵盖网络、应用、数据与人工智能、设备、知识产权和社会层面的多层威胁分析框架，识别直播音乐内容、表达性互动数据、身份与会话元数据及知识产权等关键资产；同时开展利益相关者调研，访谈来自13个组织的14名参与者，并评估TLS/TCP、SRTP和DTLS等协议在严格低时延条件下的适用性。

Result: 调研显示，神经生理数据泄露和实时流中断被认为是最关键风险，其次是知识产权侵权和虚拟化身冒用。TLS over TCP通常难以满足实时音乐互动的低时延要求，而面向流传输的轻量机制如SRTP和DTLS在安全性与性能之间具有更好的平衡。

Conclusion: 音乐元宇宙应采用安全内生设计，重点实施时延感知的安全机制、互动路径差异化保护、数据最小化和边缘侧处理，以在保障信任、隐私与合规的同时维持实时音乐交互性能。

Abstract: The Musical Metaverse (MM) introduces immersive, real-time environments for collaborative musical interaction, characterized by ultra-low-latency constraints, continuous multimodal data streams, and heterogeneous devices. These properties create a distinctive security and privacy landscape that differs significantly from conventional XR or multimedia systems. This paper presents a multi-layer threat analysis of MM ecosystems, identifying key assets including live musical content, expressive interaction data, identity and session metadata, and intellectual property. Threats are analyzed across network, application, data/AI, device, intellectual property rights, and social layers, with particular attention to risks arising from expressive and neurophysiological data, which enable inference, re-identification, and potential privacy violations. We describe a stakeholder-driven survey involving 14 participants from 13 organizations, revealing that neurophysiological data leakage and real-time stream disruption are perceived as the most critical risks, followed by intellectual property infringement and avatar impersonation. We further evaluate the suitability of existing security protocols under strict latency constraints, showing that conventional approaches such as TLS over TCP are often incompatible with real-time musical interaction, while lightweight, stream-oriented mechanisms (e.g., SRTP, DTLS) provide a more suitable balance between security and performance. Based on these findings, we derive a set of design guidelines for MM systems, emphasizing latency-aware security, differentiation of interaction paths, data minimization, and edge-centric processing. The results support a security-by-design approach that enables trust and compliance without compromising real-time performance.

</details>


### [109] [PatchBench: Evaluating AI Agents for Vulnerability Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04075)
*Chihao Shen,Jiacheng Li,Aastha Mahajan,Jeffery Siyuan Tian,Yonghwi Kwon,Yizheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出，现有基于PoC崩溃是否消失的漏洞修复评测存在严重有效性问题：约25%的智能体补丁与历史开发者补丁高度相似，且许多补丁仅通过修改崩溃栈附近代码来压制崩溃而未修复根因。为此，作者提出PatchBench，通过漏洞移植、代码变异及更全面的安全性和语义正确性验证，提升C/C++漏洞修复评测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测主要检查原始PoC是否仍能触发崩溃，无法区分真正修复、历史补丁记忆复现和表层崩溃抑制。这会高估智能体的漏洞修复能力，并削弱评测结果对真实场景的解释力。

Method: 作者提出补丁相似度指标，用于识别智能体补丁与历史开发者补丁之间的相似性；分析智能体是否利用崩溃栈位置进行表层修复。进一步构建PatchBench，选择真实修复位置位于崩溃栈之外的漏洞，并通过漏洞移植和代码变异将历史漏洞迁移到新的仓库上下文中，以降低补丁记忆和针对基准结构投机的风险。同时设计覆盖安全性与语义正确性的补丁验证方法，并在11个先进智能体上进行评测。

Result: 平均25%的智能体补丁与历史开发者补丁存在较高相似度，表明补丁记忆是现实且重要的评测威胁。仅使用PoC验证会使智能体的漏洞修复任务解决率平均被高估1.83倍。研究还发现，智能体经常通过修改崩溃栈相关代码来抑制崩溃，而不是定位并修复漏洞根因。

Conclusion: 漏洞修复智能体的现有性能评估明显受到补丁记忆和表层修复的影响。PatchBench通过改变漏洞上下文并强化安全性、语义正确性验证，为更可靠、更接近真实场景的C/C++自动漏洞修复评测提供了基准，也说明未来研究应重点提升根因定位、泛化修复和语义保持能力。

Abstract: AI agents have recently demonstrated strong performance in automated vulnerability patching. However, existing evaluations often validate a patch only by testing whether the provided Proof-of-Concept (PoC) input still triggers a crash. This leaves two key threats to validity: agents may reproduce memorized historical developer patches, or they may generate surface-level fixes that only suppress the reported crash.
  We study these concerns for C/C++ vulnerability patching. We introduce a patch similarity metric to detect memorized patches. On average, 25% of the agent patches exhibit substantial similarity to historical developer patches, indicating that patch memorization is a real threat to the validity of vulnerability patching evaluations. Meanwhile, agents also frequently exploit benchmark structures to pass patch validation by patching on the crash stack trace to suppress the crash, rather than localizing and fixing the root cause of the vulnerabilities.
  To handle these issues, we propose PatchBench, a new benchmark for evaluating AI agents on realistic vulnerability patching tasks. PatchBench selects vulnerabilities whose ground-truth fixes lie outside the crash stack and uses vulnerability transplant and code mutations to migrate historical vulnerabilities into new repository contexts, reducing the risks of surface-level fixes and patch memorization. We develop new patch validation methods that thoroughly evaluate both security and semantic correctness of agent patches. Across 11 state-of-the-art agents, including the top three AIxCC agents, the original PoC-only validation inflates the patching task solve rate of agents by 1.83$\times$ on average. Our results reveal key limitations of current patching agents and point to future research directions for more reliable vulnerability repair.

</details>


### [110] [AlcaTRAz - Anchored Tree-Rule Defense Against Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03693)
*Jakub Reš,Petr Kaška,Martin Perešíni,Martin Ukrop,Kamil Malinka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AlcaTRAz，一种仅依赖输入文本的提示级防御方法，通过字符级扰动破坏越狱提示的结构规律，在不修改或重训练目标模型的情况下提升安全性，并较好保持正常任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱防御方法常需访问模型权重或内部状态，难以部署于黑盒模型。研究旨在设计一种可迁移、无需模型内部信息且兼顾安全性与实用性的输入级防御方案。

Method: AlcaTRAz基于规则树自动学习可迁移的转换规则，在输入文本的选定位置插入受控字符级扰动，以削弱越狱攻击所依赖的结构模式。实验覆盖33个开放权重模型、22类越狱攻击和短单轮正常问题，并与Llama Guard、RA-LLM及Goal Prioritization三种提示级基线比较。

Result: 在模型—攻击组合中，AlcaTRAz有73.4%的综合安全性与功能性得分最佳。防御后总体得分的众数由未防御时的10降至2，表明恶意请求更接近拒答；正常问题平均得分为8.35，仅比未防御基线8.62低0.27分。

Conclusion: AlcaTRAz能显著降低越狱成功率，同时较好保留正常使用性能，适合黑盒部署并可作为纵深防御的一层。但该方法无法完全消除高危响应，且未评估自适应攻击者，因此不能被视为单独的安全保证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that bypass safety alignment through carefully crafted prompts. Many existing defenses require access to model weights or internals, making them difficult to apply to black-box deployments. We propose AlcaTRAz (Anchored Tree-Rule defense Against jailbreaks), a prompt-level defense based on rule trees that operates exclusively on the input text and requires no modification or retraining of the target model. The method automatically learns a transferable transformation rule that inserts controlled character-level perturbations at selected positions, thereby disrupting structural regularities exploited by jailbreak attacks while largely preserving the model's utility on benign queries. We evaluate the proposed method across 33 open-weight models, 22 jailbreak attack types, and a benchmark of short, single-turn benign questions, comparing against three representative prompt-level baselines (Llama Guard, RA-LLM, Goal Prioritization). Among the compared defenses, AlcaTRAz achieves the best composite security and functionality score in 73.4 % of model-attack combinations and shifts the aggregate score from a modal value of 10 (maximal-severity response to the malicious request) in the undefended setting to a modal value of 2 (near-refusal) after defense, while keeping the mean benign score within 0.27 points of the undefended baseline (8.35 vs. 8.62 on a 0-10 scale). AlcaTRAz substantially reduces but does not eliminate jailbreak success: a high-severity tail remains, and we do not consider adaptive attackers, so we position it as one layer within a defense-in-depth strategy rather than a standalone guarantee.

</details>


### [111] [Rent-a-RAG: Embedding-Space Watermarks for Auditing Third-Party RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03749)
*Alexandr Goultiaev Tolstokorov,Kyriakos Mouratidis,Javad Dogani,Nikolaos Laoutaris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出DirBucket：一种面向第三方多提供方RAG市场的提供方侧语义水印与黑盒审计框架。它通过在文档释义改写中注入与提供方专属秘密方向对齐的嵌入偏置，使数据提供方可仅依据RAG系统的外部回答，统计检测特定文档是否被违规复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方RAG运营方可能在授权到期或未补偿的情况下继续使用数据提供方语料，但提供方通常无法访问检索索引、模型内部状态或引用链。现有审计面临三项困难：运营方不合作、生成回答会改写原文、单个回答可能融合多个提供方的证据。因此需要一种不依赖内部访问、能处理混合来源回答且不显著损害检索效用的审计机制。

Method: DirBucket对提供方文档进行语义保持的释义改写，并令其嵌入表示向该提供方对应的秘密“桶方向”产生偏置。审计时，提供方向目标RAG系统提出查询并收集黑盒回答，再依据回答的嵌入统计特征判断目标提供方水印是否被激活。该设计旨在将文档级复用信号保留至生成结果中，同时利用提供方专属方向区分目标来源和非目标来源。

Result: 在模拟多提供方混合复用、仅允许黑盒访问的主要基准上，DirBucket是唯一能够稳定获得较强目标检测能力且不触发非目标提供方的比较方法；其在23个被审计回答中均检测到不合规复用。文中还报告，该水印可抵抗生成后答案清洗，所评估的规避策略均无法同时消除检测信号并维持用户感知的回答质量。方法无需修改即可迁移至由真实临床、网络威胁情报和法律语料构建的第二基准。

Conclusion: DirBucket表明，基于嵌入空间的提供方专属语义水印可将第三方RAG中的文档复用转化为可统计验证的黑盒审计问题。其核心贡献是兼顾语义保真、检索可用性、来源区分与对生成后改写的鲁棒性；但摘要未说明水印对不同嵌入模型、检索器配置、长时序自适应规避及大规模实际部署成本的敏感性，这些仍需在全文实验中进一步核验。

Abstract: Third-party retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) marketplaces create a new auditing problem: data providers may license corpora to a RAG operator, yet later have no visibility into whether their documents are being reused without compensation. Auditing this misuse is difficult because the operator is non-cooperative, answers are paraphrased by the generator, and one response may combine evidence from many providers. We propose DirBucket, a provider-side semantic watermarking and black-box auditing framework for document-level reuse in multi-provider RAG. DirBucket watermarks documents by meaning-preserving paraphrases whose embeddings are biased toward provider-bucket secret directions, enabling detection from black-box answers while preserving retrieval utility. On a challenging benchmark that reflects mixed-provider reuse under black-box access, DirBucket is the only method that consistently achieves strong target detection with no non-target activation, detecting non-compliance in every audit within 23 audited answers on our primary benchmark. The watermark survives adversarial post-answer laundering, and none of the evaluated evasion strategies simultaneously defeats detection while preserving user-perceived answer quality. Detection transfers unchanged to a second benchmark built from real clinical, cyber-threat-intelligence, and legal provider corpora. These results suggest that embedding-space watermarking can make document reuse in third-party RAG statistically auditable.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond the Trust Boundary: A Critical Reassessment of the FIDO2 Threat Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03789)
*Aditya Mitra,Kolluru Sai Abhiram,Sibi Chakkaravarthy Sethuraman,Anitha S*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新评估现实部署环境下的FIDO2/WebAuthn安全性，指出其安全保障不仅取决于密码学实现，还依赖浏览器、操作系统、硬件、协议栈及元数据基础设施的可信性。研究分析了八类攻击向量，并表明跨层攻击可能削弱甚至绕过FIDO2的预期安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FIDO2通常因采用公钥密码学和硬件支持认证器而被视为具备抗钓鱼能力，但这一判断往往忽略了实际部署中的环境假设。本文旨在扩展传统密码学威胁模型，评估非密码学组件及供应链、设备和运行环境对FIDO2安全性的影响。

Method: 研究将威胁模型扩展至FIDO2完整技术栈，系统考察恶意浏览器扩展、平台处理程序恶意软件、被动嗅探、虚拟设备驱动、CTAP2专用恶意软件、USB或硬件植入、恶意USB集线器及扩展设备、NFC中继攻击等八类攻击向量。同时分析AAGUID与时序信息造成的用户画像风险、浏览器或操作系统受害后的安全影响、跨层攻击链，以及针对FIDO Metadata Service（MDS3）和设备证明机制的攻击，并按照权限、技能和资源需求对攻击进行分类。

Result: 研究发现，FIDO2依赖的多项环境假设在现实中可能不成立。AAGUID和时序信息可被用于用户画像及定向攻击；浏览器、操作系统或硬件遭到控制时，即使底层密码学原语未被攻破，FIDO2的安全性仍可能受到破坏。多个攻击向量还可组合形成跨层攻击链，绕过预期的认证保护，并影响设备证明及作为元数据根信任的MDS3。

Conclusion: FIDO2部署的主要薄弱点通常不在密码学层，而在其周围的可信环境。有效防护需要采用分层缓解措施，覆盖浏览器、操作系统、硬件、协议栈、外设连接及元数据基础设施，而不能仅依赖认证器和密码学机制本身。

Abstract: FIDO2/WebAuthn has been widely deployed as a phishing-resistant authentication scheme. Because FIDO2 relies on public-key cryptography and hardware-backed authenticators, its security is often assumed to be guaranteed by design, provided that the cryptographic implementation is correct. In this work, we critically reassess the FIDO2 threat model and show that several commonly assumed security properties do not hold under realistic deployment conditions. We extend the threat model beyond the cryptographic layer to examine eight attack vectors across the FIDO2 stack: malicious browser extensions, platform-handler malware, passive sniffing, virtual device drivers, CTAP2-specific malware, USB/hardware implants, malicious USB hubs/docks/extenders, and NFC relay attacks. Our analysis shows that FIDO2 depends on environmental assumptions that may not hold in practice. We demonstrate how AAGUID and timing information can enable user profiling and targeted attacks, and how compromise of the browser, operating system, or hardware can undermine FIDO2 security even when the underlying cryptographic primitives remain uncompromised. We further show that attack chains spanning multiple layers can bypass the intended security guarantees of FIDO2. These findings indicate that the primary weakness in a FIDO2 deployment is often not the cryptographic layer, but the surrounding trusted environment. We also examine how these attack vectors can undermine device attestation by targeting the FIDO Metadata Service (MDS3), which serves as a root of trust for authenticator metadata. Finally, we characterize the attacks according to privilege, skill, and resource requirements. We conclude that effective FIDO2 security requires layered mitigations covering the browser, operating system, hardware, protocol stack, and metadata infrastructure.

</details>


### [113] [Inferring Hidden User Models from the Behavior of Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03815)
*Haoyang Li,Yaxin Xiao,Qingqing Ye,Huadi Zheng,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出UMPeek，一种针对个性化大语言模型智能体的黑盒隐私推断攻击。即使原始记忆文本和后端状态均不可访问，攻击者仍可通过观察用户模型影响下的可见决策，恢复其中保留的私密语义信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统常将原始记忆压缩为结构化“用户模型”，并认为删除可直接访问的源文本即可降低隐私泄露风险。论文指出，这一假设忽略了用户模型对后续响应与选择的行为影响：隐藏状态虽然不可读，但其语义可能经由系统输出被间接推断，因此需要评估新的行为侧信道风险。

Method: UMPeek采用假设驱动的自适应探测框架：首先根据请求中未被明确限定的选择空间构造关于用户偏好、属性或历史信息的候选假设；随后在看似正常的后续任务之间切换，通过模型的个性化选择获得证据；仅保留得到可见行为支持且未被后续行为反驳的推断。论文在多种个性化任务和用户模型后端上进行基准测试，并在真实系统中使用已确认被保留的信息验证攻击，同时评估针对自适应探测的防御。

Result: 实验表明，UMPeek在基准环境及真实系统比较中均优于已有攻击方法。即便部署仅针对输出内容的防御措施，UMPeek仍能恢复部分用户信息，说明这类防御难以消除由个性化决策行为带来的泄露。

Conclusion: 将原始记录和内部后端状态设为不可访问，并不足以保证语义隐私。只要隐藏用户模型持续塑造可观察的决策，攻击者就可能利用自适应交互推断其中的信息。个性化智能体的隐私防护应从保护存储内容扩展到限制、检测或缓解行为层面的推断泄露。

Abstract: Recent personalized LLM agents increasingly transform information retained in memory into compressed or structured representations, which we call user models, to guide later decisions. When source wording is removed from the state reachable through the ordinary interface, these models are commonly treated as more privacy-preserving because direct memory-extraction attacks lose the text they target. Yet we argue that user models expose a new attack surface because an attacker can still recover the private information from the personalized choices they shape, even when source records and backend state remain inaccessible. We therefore introduce UMPeek, a black-box attack based on hypothesis-guided adaptive probing to infer such hidden user model. It forms hypotheses from choices left open by a request, switches among ordinary follow-up tasks, and retains only claims supported and not contradicted by visible behavior. We conduct an extensive benchmark evaluation across diverse personalization tasks and user-model backends against existing attacks. We further validate UMPeek in real-world systems using information confirmed to be retained, and we evaluate defenses against its adaptive probing. Overall, UMPeek outperforms existing attacks in both benchmark and real-world comparisons and continues to recover user information under response-level defenses, showing that keeping records and backend state inaccessible does not guarantee semantic privacy when retained information shapes visible behavior.

</details>


### [114] [Flip, Don't Shuffle: Watermarking LLMs at the Speed of Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03844)
*Simone Ceppi,Ignacio Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出无状态伯努利水印（SBW），通过对每个词元独立执行伯努利试验确定绿色列表归属，以 O(1) 复杂度和单内核执行实现高效大语言模型水印。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法依赖词表置换、多层候选比较或中间张量分配，带来较高计算与存储开销，并限制了全词表自盐化和分布式推理兼容性。本文旨在降低水印推理成本，同时保持既有检测统计保证。

Method: SBW使用基于计数器的随机数生成器，对每个词元进行一次比较来决定绿色列表成员资格。作者证明其零假设下的 z 分数仍服从标准正态分布，并评估两种播种方案、八组参数配置及不同哈希函数。实验还比较了全词表自盐化、端到端生成开销、检测性能和文本多样性。

Result: SBW在所有批大小下的端到端开销低于1%，支持单内核执行和分布式推理。全词表自盐化速度较KGW快超过6000倍，较SynthID快约2倍。GPU原生Jenkins哈希使零假设校准改善1.8倍，并生成更多样化文本。不同配置下与基线具有统计等价性，ROC-AUC差异低于0.01。

Conclusion: SBW在保持固定大小绿色列表检测保证的同时，显著降低了计算和内存开销，为高吞吐、全词表自盐化及分布式部署提供了实用方案。研究还表明，哈希函数设计是影响水印质量和统计校准的重要因素。

Abstract: We introduce Stateless Bernoulli Watermarking (SBW), a new statistical watermark for Large Language Models that determines green list membership through independent per-token Bernoulli trials. Unlike KGW's vocabulary permutation or SynthID's multi-layer tournament, SBW requires only a single comparison per token against a counter-based random number generator, reducing membership complexity to $O(1)$ and enabling single-kernel execution with zero intermediate allocations. We prove that this formulation preserves the same detection guarantees as fixed-size green lists: the z-score test remains $\mathcal{N}(0,1)$ under the null. The stateless architecture enables capabilities unavailable to existing methods: full-vocabulary self-salt watermarking (over 6000$\times$ faster than KGW's self-salt and 2$\times$ faster than SynthID despite biasing the entire vocabulary with candidate-dependent seeding) and architectural compatibility with distributed inference. In end-to-end generation benchmarks, SBW adds less than 1\% overhead at all batch sizes. We additionally identify hash function design as a previously unexplored axis for watermark quality, showing that a GPU-native Jenkins hash improves null calibration by 1.8$\times$ while producing more diverse text. Experiments across two seeding schemes and eight $(γ, δ)$ configurations confirm statistical equivalence with ROC-AUC differences below 0.01.

</details>


### [115] [NACRE: Rethinking Confidential Containers through Native Architectural Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03849)
*Linke Song,Wenhao Wang,Weijie Liu,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NACRE是一种面向RISC-V的软硬件协同方案，将动态的Linux容器进程组作为硬件保护单元，在保留主机资源管理能力的同时隔离容器机密状态。原型性能开销较低，系统调用和管道指标最大偏差不超过3.5%，nginx聚合吞吐仅下降1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux容器共享主机内核，虽然具备高密度和快速生命周期管理优势，但被攻陷的主机仍可能检查或篡改容器状态。现有机密计算方案通常保护单个 enclave 地址空间或完整虚拟机，近期的容器级方案也往往引入独立保护上下文，无法直接将由主机管理的动态Linux进程组作为体系结构保护单元。

Method: NACRE通过RISC-V硬件与软件协同实现原生机密容器。其核心设计是分离主机的资源管理权限与访问或提交受保护状态的权限：硬件识别容器身份，并将受保护陷阱导向隔离的S模式代理；M模式监控器负责提交安全敏感的身份、地址映射和页面状态转换。代理在不修改satp的情况下向主机Linux委托服务；对于既不访问私有数据、也不修改受保护状态的服务，则无需进入M模式。研究团队基于QEMU、OpenSBI、Linux、可信代理和runc实现原型，并覆盖单容器私有内存、启动、缺页、fork/COW、用户访问和销毁路径。

Result: 在五项lmbench系统调用与管道指标上，三次运行均值相对runc基线的差异不超过3.5%。对八组不同nginx对象大小的吞吐均值进行等权汇总后，整体吞吐下降1.9%。

Conclusion: NACRE证明了将动态主机管理型Linux容器进程组直接作为硬件保护单元的可行性，在保持主机资源管理灵活性的同时提供容器状态保护，并实现了较低的性能开销。当前原型主要验证单容器私有内存基础机制，保护范围和多容器场景仍有待进一步评估。

Abstract: Linux containers achieve high density and fast lifecycle operations by sharing the host kernel, but this design also lets a compromised host inspect or modify container state. Existing
  confidential-computing systems protect an enclave address space or an entire guest operating system, while recent container-granularity systems still add a separate protection context.
  These abstractions do not make a dynamic group of host-managed Linux processes the architectural protection unit.
  This paper presents NACRE, a RISC-V hardware-software co-design for native confidential containers. Its key insight is to separate the host's authority to manage resources from its
  authority to access or commit protected state. Hardware-recognized container identities direct protected traps to an isolated S-mode agent, while an M-mode monitor commits security-
  sensitive identity, mapping, and page transitions. The agent delegates services to host Linux without changing satp; services that neither access private bytes nor modify protected
  state also avoid M-mode. We prototype NACRE by extending QEMU, OpenSBI, Linux, a trusted agent, and runc. The prototype implements the single-container private-memory substrate and
  covered launch, fault, fork/COW, user-access, and teardown paths. Across five lmbench syscall and pipe metrics, the three-run means remain within 3.5% of the runc-origin baseline. With
  the eight nginx object-size means weighted equally, aggregate throughput is 1.9% lower.

</details>


### [116] [A Blind Trust, the Bloody Thrust: When Attacker-Controlled Hook Updates Steer AI Agent Harnesses towards Malicious Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03884)
*Pengxun Li,Litian Zhang,Jianwei Hou,Shujiang Wu,Song Li,Zifeng Kang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了AI智能体运行框架中的生命周期钩子更新机制存在供应链安全风险：攻击者可仅通过插件元数据和钩子配置更新，将原本良性的插件转化为在宿主机上自动执行非预期命令的恶意组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI智能体框架允许在会话启动、工具调用和文件编辑等事件上绑定Shell命令。这些命令通常具有宿主机权限，且可能在大模型不可见的时机触发。论文指出，框架对生命周期钩子更新内容的盲目信任形成了此前被忽视的攻击面。

Method: 作者提出开源自动化攻击框架HookPry，在供应链威胁模型下系统测试异构AI智能体框架：攻击者不需要控制运行环境或插件代码主体，仅控制插件元数据与生命周期钩子配置。HookPry通过更新将攻击者指定命令绑定到看似正常的运行事件，并覆盖十类攻击目标。

Result: 在25种框架与后端组合、共1,000次端到端实验中，HookPry成功影响全部7个被测框架，单个框架的最高成功率达到92.5%。防御评估显示，Microsoft Defender的检出召回率为0%；三种静态防御手段的并集仍遗漏47.5%的恶意样本。

Conclusion: 论文表明，生命周期钩子配置应被视为高权限可执行代码，而非普通元数据。现有检测机制对这类配置驱动型供应链风险覆盖不足；框架需要加强钩子更新的来源验证、完整性校验、权限隔离、显式审计与运行时行为约束。

Abstract: Modern AI agent harnesses expose lifecycle hooks that bind shell commands to runtime events such as session start, tool calls, and file edits. These commands run with host privileges yet ship as lifecycle-hook configuration and may fire at times the LLM never observes. We identify the lifecycle-hook update path, which harnesses trust blindly, as a new attack surface. Under a supply-chain threat model in which an attacker controls only plugin metadata and lifecycle-hook configuration, a benign versioned plugin can be trojanized by an update that silently binds attacker-chosen commands to benign events, yielding malicious host-side behavior such as privilege escalation. We propose HookPry, an open-source and fully automated attack framework that systematically exploits this vulnerability across heterogeneous AI agent harnesses. HookPry realizes ten attack objectives; across 25 combinations of harnesses and backends in 1,000 end-to-end runs, it compromises all seven evaluated harnesses, with per-harness success rates reaching 92.5%. Representative defenses remain insufficient: Microsoft Defender has 0% recall, and the union of three static defenses misses 47.5% of malicious artifacts.

</details>


### [117] [Practice Makes (Im)Perfect: A Look Back at Benchmarking Practices for Microarchitectural Side-Channel Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03893)
*Iliana Fayolle,Antoine Geimer,Daniel De Almeida Braga,Clémentine Maurice*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统审查了2014—2024年发表于顶级安全与体系结构会议的83篇微架构侧信道攻击论文，识别出19类重复出现的基准测试缺陷。平均每篇论文存在5.5项缺陷，表明现有评估在完整性、相关性、有效性和可复现性方面普遍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常使用隐蔽信道带宽、针对简单AES或RSA实现的密钥恢复等间接指标，并沿用早期论文的基准结果。然而，微架构攻击对硬件、软件和实验配置极其敏感，系统的细微变化就可能显著影响结果，导致比较缺乏公平性和可复现性，也难以准确反映新攻击或新原语的关键能力。

Method: 作者对2014—2024年顶级安全与体系结构会议中的83篇微架构侧信道攻击论文进行系统调研，建立并定义了19类基准测试缺陷，涵盖评估完整性、相关性、有效性和可复现性。随后统计各类缺陷的出现频率，分析不同年份的发展趋势，并比较安全会议与体系结构会议的评估实践。

Result: 每篇论文平均存在5.5项基准测试缺陷。主要问题包括缺少公平比较或完全不比较、未公开代码与实验材料，以及未评估攻击的关键属性。研究还发现，即使在高选择性会议中，这些问题仍然普遍存在，并且不同研究领域和年份之间呈现出一定的实践差异。

Conclusion: 微架构侧信道研究亟需更严格、针对性更强且可复现的基准测试规范。评估新攻击时，应围绕其核心属性选择相关指标，明确实验条件，提供代码和材料，并进行公平、完整的对比，以提升研究结论的可信度和可重复性。

Abstract: Microarchitectural side-channel research has grown at an exceptional pace in recent years, increasing the need for rigorous and meaningful benchmarking. Early attack papers typically relied on indirect proxies, such as covert-channel bandwidth or key-recovery on naive AES and RSA implementations, setting de facto standards that many subsequent works continued to replicate, sometimes by directly comparing against raw numbers from prior work. While these practices offer convenient points of comparison, current benchmarks may not be the most relevant to assess specific properties of new primitives. Even more problematic, microarchitectural attacks are notoriously sensitive to experimental conditions: minimal changes in the target system can significantly alter outcomes and performance. As a result, inadequate evaluation practices undermine reproducibility and cast doubt on the relevance of comparisons, even in top-tier venues where such issues should be identified. This paper tackles the core problem of proper benchmarking for microarchitectural side-channel attacks and examines its broader impact on research quality in the field. We survey 83 attack papers published in top-ranked security and architecture conferences from 2014 to 2024. From this corpus, we identify and define 19 recurrent benchmarking flaws that affect evaluation completeness, relevance, soundness, and reproducibility. These flaws include unfair or absent comparisons, missing code or materials, and the failure to evaluate the key attack properties. On average, each paper exhibits 5.5 such flaws, highlighting how widespread the issue is, even in highly selective venues. Based on our findings, we identify and suggest key properties that are relevant to properly evaluate new attacks. We also highlight trends over time and different practices between security and architecture conferences.

</details>


### [118] [A Black Box for Agentic Processes: Blockchain-Anchored Evidence for AI Agent Communication, Human Oversight, and GRC Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04017)
*Arslan Brömme*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向自主AI代理流程的厂商中立黑盒审计架构，通过区块链锚定的密码学承诺记录关键通信、人工审批、工具调用和过程产物，以增强事后核验能力，但不将敏感内容直接上链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多代理工作流涉及代理间通信、工具调用、结果交换和人工批准，现有机制难以可靠重建事件发生时间、参与主体、适用控制及记录是否被篡改。因此，组织需要一种兼顾隐私、完整性和合规审计的证据体系。

Method: 构建黑盒式证据架构，对选定的代理通信、人工审批、工具调用和过程文件生成密码学承诺，并进行区块链时间锚定。论文进一步定义证据模型，区分时间锚定、产物完整性、事件排序、采集真实性、授权锚定和因果可追溯性，并提出面向GRC的证据选择、合规测试、证据流监控、事件重建和监管报告支持机制。

Result: 论文主要给出概念模型和架构设计，未提供实证性能测试或安全性评估。该方法可增强关键流程记录的完整性和事后验证基础，但区块链锚定本身无法证明事件采集真实、锚定行为获得授权、事件之间存在正确因果关系，也不能证明内容的语义真实性或阻止代理行为失当。

Conclusion: 区块链锚定的密码学证据适合作为自主代理审计体系中的补充控制，可提升记录防篡改性、时间证明能力和合规准备度。要实现完整审计，还必须结合可信采集、访问控制、事件排序、权限治理和因果追踪等架构措施；本文的贡献定位于立场与架构框架，而非经过验证的工程系统。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly communicate with other agents, invoke tools, exchange intermediate results, and request human approvals. These workflows create a new auditability problem: organizations must reconstruct what happened, when it happened, which agent or human was involved, which control or policy applied, and whether records were modified afterwards. Motivated by the 2026 OpenAI/Hugging Face incident, this position and architecture paper proposes a product- and vendor-neutral black-box architecture for agentic processes. The architecture creates blockchain-anchored cryptographic commitments for selected agent communications, human-in-the-loop approvals, tool calls, and process artifacts without placing sensitive content on-chain. We define an evidence model that distinguishes temporal anchoring and artifact integrity from event ordering, capture authenticity, authorized anchoring, and causal traceability. The latter properties require additional architectural controls. We then discuss practical use for Governance, Risk, and Compliance (GRC), including compliance testing, risk-based evidence selection, monitoring evidence streams, incident reconstruction, and regulatory reporting readiness under the EU AI Act, NIS2, and the Cyber Resilience Act (CRA). This position and architecture paper does not present an empirical performance or security evaluation. The approach does not prevent agent misbehavior or prove semantic truth. Rather, it strengthens the evidentiary basis for later verification of critical process traces.

</details>


### [119] [AI-Assisted Design of a Post-Quantum Cryptographic Accelerator: A Deployed-Silicon Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04058)
*Jungmin Park,Eunha Kim,Wooseop Kim,Seongjoon Cho,Byungho Cha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向ML-DSA硬件加速器的新型验收方法：以字节级精确的黄金参考实现结合随机对抗性压力测试，覆盖传统已知答案测试（KAT）难以触发的拒绝重采样路径。实验中完成301,343次数据相关签名且零缺陷逃逸，并报告了由智能体大语言模型驱动的ML-KEM-768与ML-DSA-65加速器开发结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KAT使用固定种子，无法充分覆盖ML-DSA签名中由消息和候选向量范数约束决定的可变拒绝循环。论文指出，某个范数检查因超出块RAM时延而未验证候选的最终系数，导致缺陷在第5次拒绝循环才暴露，因此需要不依赖固定测试向量、直接检验最终工件行为的验收机制。

Method: 构建字节级精确的黄金参考模型，并结合随机化、对抗性压力测试持续驱动签名拒绝循环；同时记录232次实验，使用智能体大语言模型完成统一ML-KEM-768和ML-DSA-65加速器从RTL设计到PCIe调试的流程，在Kintex-7 XC7K160T上实现片上密钥托管。通过覆盖全部六项FIPS操作及779,945项检查，对部署基线进行一致性与可靠性验证。

Result: 新验收门完成301,343次数据相关签名测试，零缺陷逃逸；部署基线在779,945项检查中同样零失败。智能体大语言模型总体成功率为71.6%，其中文档与研究任务成功率为77%至85%，综合成与硬件调试任务为50%至53%。最终实现的硬件占用率为98.5%，并在全部六项FIPS操作上与参考结果字节级一致。

Conclusion: 固定KAT不足以验证包含数据相关拒绝循环的密码硬件；黄金参考模型加随机对抗性测试能够显著缩小该验收盲区。结果表明，即使生成者可靠性有限，只要验收机制独立、可重复且直接判断工件行为，也能验证最终实现的功能一致性。不过，摘要中的成功率和零失败结论主要适用于所述实验配置，不能单独证明所有设计场景或更广泛硬件实现的安全性。

Abstract: Post-quantum migration is mandated on published timelines, and silicon that ships with a defect cannot be patched remotely. The standard acceptance gate cannot detect an entire class of ML-DSA defects. Signing resamples until a candidate meets its norm bounds, so the executed path varies with the message, whereas known-answer tests (KATs) sample fixed values and reach only the depths their seeds trigger. Our accelerator passed its full KAT regression while carrying a norm check that outran block-RAM latency, leaving each candidate's final coefficients unverified; the escape surfaced at reject-loop iteration 5. The blind spot lies in the instrument, not the engineer; care cannot remove it. We replace that gate. A byte-exact golden-reference oracle paired with randomized adversarial soak drives the rejection loop past any fixed vector, closing the gap: 301,343 data-dependent signings, zero escapes. Because the gate judges artifacts and never authors, trust becomes separable from authorship, making AI authorship an answerable question. We report 232 logged experiments in which an agentic large language model drove a unified ML-KEM-768 and ML-DSA-65 accelerator with on-chip key custody from RTL to PCIe bring-up on one Kintex-7 XC7K160T, shipped at 98.5% slice occupancy. Success was 71.6%, following a hardware-coupling gradient, 77-85% for documentation and research against 50-53% for synthesis and bring-up, which observability can explain: failure concentrates where corrective signals are physical-side only. That so unreliable an author produced an artifact byte-exact across all six FIPS operations -- its deployed baseline surviving the same 779,945-check zero-failure soak -- is the claim.

</details>


### [120] [SENTINEL-RL: Offloading Topological Reasoning from LLM Agents in the Security Operations Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04159)
*Uday Vallabhaneni,Cassie L. Cagwin,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sentinel-RL提出一种面向企业级SOC的智能体架构，将图拓扑推理与语义推理解耦：图注意力编码器压缩认证子图，PPO策略生成受约束的调查动作，LLM仅负责生成经批评器审核的分析叙述。实验显示其在数据加载、告警响应、检测性能和闭环延迟方面均具备较好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全运营中心智能体面临两项关键限制：有限上下文窗口难以容纳大规模多主机认证图；自由文本生成无法保证处置建议符合实际网络拓扑。研究目标是提升企业级SOC分析的可扩展性、动作一致性、可审计性与人工控制能力。

Method: 系统采用异构图注意力编码器，将实时认证子图映射为固定维度状态；使用PPO策略将状态映射到受约束的调查动作集合；LLM智能体仅消费策略建议并生成面向分析师的叙述，且输出需经过批评器门控。工程上采用两阶段CREATE数据导入、滑动窗口告警机制、热节点死锁规避方案及锚节点共置的HPC部署模式，并保留人工审批边界。

Result: 在LANL综合多源网络安全事件数据集和Indiana University Quartz HPC集群上，24M条认证边在单台32核节点上的Neo4j导入耗时14.2分钟，约为MERGE流程的1/24；25事件/10秒阈值在50次试验中均可于不超过2.5秒触发；PPO训练200轮后平均回报为8.74±0.31，留出集精确率为0.91、召回率为0.87；完整的检测—调查—建议—人工批准闭环中位耗时为6.3秒。

Conclusion: Sentinel-RL通过将拓扑约束交由图模型和强化学习策略处理、将语言模型限制在受控叙述环节，缓解了LLM直接执行SOC任务时的上下文容量和动作可靠性问题。结果表明该架构具备较好的工程性能与检测效果，但其企业部署价值仍需结合误报成本、策略泛化能力、可逆处置保证和长期真实环境验证进一步评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as autonomous SOC analysts, but two limitations make them unreliable at enterprise scale: a finite context window cannot hold a multi-thousand-host authentication graph, and free-form generation offers no guarantee that a recommended containment action is consistent with the topology it operates on. We present Sentinel-RL, an agentic-SOC architecture that decouples topological reasoning from semantic reasoning: a heterogeneous graph attention encoder summarizes the live authentication subgraph into a fixed-dimensional state, a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy maps this state to a constrained set of investigative actions, and an LLM agent loop is restricted to consuming the policy's recommendations and producing analyst-readable narratives gated by a critic. We instantiate the system on the LANL Comprehensive, Multi-Source Cyber-Security Events dataset and the Indiana University Quartz HPC cluster, reporting four results: (i) a two-phase CREATE ingestion pattern loads a 24M-edge authentication subgraph into Neo4j in 14.2 minutes on a single 32-core node, roughly 24x faster than the canonical MERGE-based pipeline; (ii) a sliding-window alert engine reliably trips a 25-event/10-second threshold in <=2.5 s across 50 trials; (iii) PPO training over 200 iterations converges to a mean episodic return of 8.74+/-0.31, with held-out precision of 0.91 and recall of 0.87 on labeled red-team events; and (iv) the integrated containment loop completes a full detect-investigate-recommend-human-approve cycle in a median of 6.3 s. We contribute a reusable engineering pattern (the hot-node deadlock workaround), a portable HPC deployment pattern (anchor-node co-location), and an enterprise-readiness analysis covering false-positive economics, reversibility guarantees, audit compliance, and the human-approval boundary.

</details>
